===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Abyssal_Other_13.gml =====
fatigue = max(fatigue, MAX_STAMINA - stamina);

if (!isDragging)
{
    thrall_ang_force = 0.017;
    thrall_friction = 0.9;
    stamina_recharge++;
    
    if (stamina_recharge > recharge_length && stamina_recharge_uses > 0)
    {
        recharge_length += 20;
        stamina_recharge = 0;
        stamina_recharge_uses--;
        fatigue = 0;
        stamina = MAX_STAMINA;
        isDragging = 1;
    }
}

if (isDragging)
{
    if (Lure.tether_length > 1000)
    {
        target_angle = point_direction(x, y, Lure.tether_x, Lure.tether_y) - (90 * image_yscale);
        
        if ((x < Lure.tether_x && image_yscale < 0) || (y < 1494 && image_yscale > 0))
        {
            if (Girl.RealismMode)
            {
                image_yscale *= -1;
                image_angle += 180;
            }
        }
    }
    else if (Lure.tether_length < 800)
    {
        target_angle = point_direction(x, y, Lure.tether_x, Lure.tether_y) + 180;
    }
    
    thrall_ang_force = 0.017;
    thrall_friction = 0.9;
    var thrall_scalar = 1 - (abs(angle_difference(image_angle + 180, target_angle)) / 180);
    thrall_force = 20 * thrall_scalar;
}

    isDragging = 0;

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_AmbianceController_Step_0.gml =====
second = date_get_second(date_current_datetime());
minute = date_get_minute(date_current_datetime());
hour = date_get_hour(date_current_datetime());
isStorming = is_it_storming();
var rate = 0.0002;

if (is_it_night())
{
    LightingSystem.day_coefficient -= (rate * Girl.delta_fixed);
    LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient -= (rate * Girl.delta_fixed);
}
else
{
    LightingSystem.day_coefficient += rate;
    LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient += rate;
}

LightingSystem.day_coefficient = clamp(LightingSystem.day_coefficient, 0, 1);

if (!is_it_night())
{
    if (is_it_halloween())
    {
        LightingSystem.day_coefficient += (sign(0.6 - LightingSystem.day_coefficient) * 0.001);
        LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient += (sign(0.7 - LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient) * 0.001);
        
        if (is_it_storming())
        {
            LightingSystem.day_coefficient += (sign(0.3 - LightingSystem.day_coefficient) * 0.001);
            LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient += (sign(0.5 - LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient) * 0.001);
        }
    }
}

if (isStorming)
    minimumOvercast = 0;
else if (minimumOvercast < 0.3)
    minimumOvercast += 0.008;

LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient = clamp(LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient, minimumOvercast, 1);

if (!loaded)
{
    tick++;
    
    if (!is_it_night())
    {
        LightingSystem.day_coefficient = 1;
        LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient = 1;
        
        if (Girl.nightfallUnlocked)
        {
            var dawn_duration = 1 / (rate * 60);
            var time_elapsed = (AmbianceController.minute * 60) + AmbianceController.second;
            
            if (time_elapsed < dawn_duration)
            {
                LightingSystem.day_coefficient = time_elapsed / dawn_duration;
                LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient = time_elapsed / dawn_duration;
            }
        }
        
        if (is_it_halloween())
        {
            LightingSystem.day_coefficient = 0.6;
            LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient = 0.7;
        }
    }
    else
    {
        var dusk_duration = 1 / (rate * 60);
        var time_elapsed = ((AmbianceController.minute - 51) * 60) + AmbianceController.second;
        
        if (tick == 2)
        {
        }
        
        if (time_elapsed < dusk_duration)
        {
            LightingSystem.day_coefficient = 1 - (time_elapsed / dusk_duration);
            LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient = 1 - (time_elapsed / dusk_duration);
        }
    }
    
    if (tick > 2)
        loaded = 1;
}

    {
        followingLure = 0;

        target_y = start_y;
        jumpingForLure = 1;
        speed = 30;
        direction = point_direction(x, y, Lure.x, Lure.y);
    }
}
else
{
    if (Lure.state == 2 && point_distance(x, y, Lure.x, Lure.y) < 200 && y > 1294 && Lure.size > 0 && Lure.hookedFishSize == 0)
        followingLure = 1;

        followingLure = 1;

{

    vspeed += ((Lure.vspeed - vspeed) * 0.2);
    hspeed += ((Lure.x - x) * 0.5);
    vspeed += ((Lure.y - y) * 0.5);
    x += ((Lure.x - x) * 0.2);
    y += ((Lure.y - y) * 0.2);
    
    if (point_distance(x, y, Lure.x, Lure.y) < 20)
    {
        if (instance_exists(Bomb))
        {
            with (Bomb)
                event_perform(ev_other, ev_user0);
        }
        else
        {
            Lure.size = -1;
            followingLure = 0;
        }
    }
    
    if (Lure.size != 1)
        jumpingForLure = 0;
    
    if (Lure.locked)
        jumpingForLure = 0;
    
    if (x > Lure.x)
        jumpingForLure = 0;
}
else if (y > 1294)
{
    dist = point_distance(x, y, target_x, target_y);
    dir = point_direction(x, y, target_x, target_y);
    hspeed += (lengthdir_x(dist, dir) * 0.01);
    vspeed += (lengthdir_y(dist, dir) * 0.01);
    
    if (followingLure && Lure.state == 1)
        x += lengthdir_x(dist * 0.1, dir);
    
    speed *= 0.995;
    
    if (speed < 8)
        speed = 8;
    
    if (abs(vspeed) < 5)
        vspeed *= 1.1;
}

if (y < 1294)
    vspeed++;

    state = 7;

{
    target_x += lengthdir_x(10, point_direction(Girl.x, Girl.y, x, y));
    target_y += lengthdir_y(10, point_direction(Girl.x, Girl.y, x, y));
    target_y += 10;
}
else
{
    target_x = x + (sin(current_time / 5000) * 100);
    target_y = 794;
}

fish_avoid_fish(object_index, avoid_self_range, avoid_self_force);

    speed *= 0.95;

    hspeed += 1;

        {
            BoatData.unlocked[item_id] = 1;
            
            for (i = 1; i <= 2; i++)
            {
                if ((item_id + i) < (BoatData.nItems - 1))
                {
                    if (BoatData.unlocked[item_id + i] == -1)
                        BoatData.unlocked[item_id + i] = 0;

            boat_was_unlocked(item_id);

        else
        {
            instance_create(0, 0, Message_MoreMoney);
        }
    }
    
    if (BoatData.unlocked[item_id])
    {
        with (Boat)
            instance_destroy();
        
        if (BoatData.object[item_id] > 0)
            instance_create(0, 0, BoatData.object[item_id]);
    }

        nearest[1] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, Felish);

            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min))
                square_distance = fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min;

                other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
            }
        }

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Bulbfish_Other_18.gml =====
if (y < 1224)
    slide_y += 1;
else if (slide_y > 5)
    slide_y = 5;

if (y < 3294)
{
    slide_x *= 0.97;
    slide_y *= 0.97;
    
    if (!is_it_night())
        slide_y += 0.1;
}
else
{
    slide_x *= 0.99;
    slide_y *= 0.99;
}

speed *= 0.8;

if ((abs(slide_x) + abs(slide_y)) > 0)
{
    if (collision_point(x, y, Collidable, 1, 1))
    {
        if (collision_point(lamp_x, lamp_y, Collidable, 1, 1))
        {
            var attempts = 0;
            var inc = 10;
            var res = 1;
            var tx, ty;
            
            while (attempts < 20)
            {
                attempts++;
                var td = random(360);
                tx = x + lengthdir_x(attempts * inc, td);
                ty = y + lengthdir_y(attempts * inc, td);
                var ux = 0;
                var uy = 0;
                
                if (!collision_point(tx, ty, Collidable, 1, 1))
                {
                    for (var i = 0; i <= (attempts * res); i++)
                    {
                        ux = lengthdir_x(1, td);
                        uy = lengthdir_y(1, td);
                        tx = x + (ux * i * (inc / res));
                        ty = y + (uy * i * (inc / res));
                        
                        if (!collision_point(tx, ty, Collidable, 1, 1))
                            break;
                    }
                    
                    break;
                }
            }
            
            lamp_x = tx;
            lamp_y = ty;
        }
        else if (point_distance(x, y, lamp_x, lamp_y) < 1)
        {
            slide_x = 0;
            slide_y = 0;
        }
        else
        {
            x += ((lamp_x - x) * 0.5);
            y += ((lamp_y - y) * 0.5);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        lamp_x = x;
        lamp_y = y;
    }
}

x += slide_x;
y += slide_y;

    {

        }
    }

{
    if (!hold_position)
    {
        target_x = CatSwim.tracker.x;
        target_y = CatSwim.tracker.y;
    }
}
else
{
    if (Lure.state != 0 && !instance_exists(CatSwim))
    {
        target_x = Lure.x;
        target_y = Lure.y;
        atOrigin = 0;
    }
    else
    {
        target_x = Girl.x + RELATIVE_ANCHOR_X;
        
        if (!instance_exists(Boat) || Boat.wasJustMoving)
            target_y = Girl.y + RELATIVE_ANCHOR_Y;
        else
            target_y = 1294 + RELATIVE_ANCHOR_Y_FROM_SEA;
        
        if (point_distance(x, y, target_x, target_y) < 20)
        {
            atOrigin = 1;
        }
        else
        {
        }
    }
    
    if (abs(Girl.hspeed) > 0.8)
        atOrigin = 0;
}

var dist = point_distance(x, y, target_x, target_y);

if (dist < 50)
    atOrigin = 1;

if (!atOrigin)
{
    if (dist > 70 || !instance_exists(CatSwim))
    {
        x += ((target_x - x) * 0.25 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
        y += ((target_y - y) * 0.25 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    }
    else
    {
        x += ((target_x - x) * 0.01 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
        y += ((target_y - y) * 0.01 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    }
}
else
{
    x = floor(target_x * 2) / 2;
    y = floor(target_y * 2) / 2;
}

if (wantsToGrab)
{
    if (!grabbed)
    {
        if (x > (Lure.x + 10))
        {
            hspeed += ((Lure.hspeed - hspeed) * 0.4);
            vspeed += ((Lure.vspeed - vspeed) * 0.4);
            hspeed += ((Lure.x - x) * 0.01);
            vspeed += ((Lure.y - y) * 0.01);
            speed *= 0.965;
        }
        else
        {
            hspeed += (((Lure.x + 80) - x) * 0.18);
            vspeed += ((Lure.y - 30 - y) * 0.18);
            speed *= 0.685;
        }
        
        if (point_distance(x, y, Lure.x, Lure.y) < 30 && Lure.x > Girl.x)
        {
            grabbed = 1;
            timer = 28;
            hspeed = Lure.hspeed;
            vspeed = Lure.vspeed;
        }
    }
    else
    {
        timer--;
        Lure.x = x;
        Lure.y = y;
        vspeed -= 0.5;
        hspeed += 0.1;
        Lure.hspeed = hspeed;
        Lure.vspeed = vspeed;
        
        if (timer <= 0)
        {
            wantsToGrab = 0;
            grabbed = 0;
        }
    }
}
else
{
    direction += (angle_difference(direction, 20) * 0.01);
    speed += ((50 - speed) * 0.1);
    
    if (point_distance(x, y, Camera.x, Camera.y) > 1200)
        instance_destroy();
}

    hspeed = 10;

    wantsToGrab = 0;

if ((Lure.x + 200) < x)
    wantsToGrab = 0;

if (y > 1194)
{
    vspeed *= 0.5;
    vspeed--;
}

{
    with (Lure.fish)
    {
        if (state == 3 && !instance_exists(Message) && (Lure.state == 0 && Rod.state == 0) && !Girl.hide_ui)
        {
            draw_set_halign(fa_center);
            
            if (Girl.level > 0)
            {
                if (instance_exists(Boat) && catalog_index >= 0)
                {
                    if (exotic && life >= 3)
                    {
                        draw_set_font(fnt_babySmall);
                        draw_set_valign(fa_bottom);
                        draw_text_shadowed(Camera.x, Camera.y - 88, "[Exotic Variety Bonus]");
                    }

                draw_set_font(fnt_babyMedium);

            else

    }

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_CatalogData_Other_10.gml =====
t_size = 0;
t_fish_id = 0;
t_count = 0;
t_value = 0;

with (CatchQuestNewNew)
    other.weight[size, fish_id] = 0;

quest_chosen = 0;
pick_hard_quest = 1;

if (random(1) > 0.2)
    pick_hard_quest = 0;

if (!quest_chosen)
{
    i = array_height_2d(count) - 1;
    
    while (i > -1)
    {
        i_fixed = i;
        
        if (!pick_hard_quest)
        {
            i_fixed = array_height_2d(count) - 1 - i;
            
            if (random(1) > 0.6)
                i_fixed = max(i_fixed, 1);
            
            if (random(1) > 0.85)
                i_fixed = max(i_fixed, 2);
        }
        
        if (!quest_chosen)
        {
            for (j = 0; j < array_length_2d(count, i_fixed); j++)
            {
                if (name[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] == "Ghost")
                    continue;
                
                if (name[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] == "Airy")
                    continue;
                
                if (name[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] == "Maskfish")
                    continue;
                
                if (name[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] == "Underfin")
                    continue;
                
                if (name[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] == "Forebearer")
                    continue;
                
                if (name[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] == "Noxius")
                    continue;
                
                if (weight[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] > 0 && count[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] == 0)
                {
                    t_size = i_fixed;
                    t_fish_id = map[i_fixed, j];
                    t_hard = pick_hard_quest;
                    quest_chosen = 1;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        
        i--;
    }
}

if (!quest_chosen)
{
    for (h = 0; h < 3; h++)
    {
        if (!quest_chosen)
        {
            i = array_height_2d(count) - 1;
            
            while (i > -1)
            {
                i_fixed = i;
                
                if (!pick_hard_quest)
                    i_fixed = array_height_2d(count) - 1 - i;
                
                if (!quest_chosen)
                {
                    for (j = 0; j < array_length_2d(count, i_fixed); j++)
                    {
                        if (weight[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] > 0 && life[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]] <= h && !hide[i_fixed, map[i_fixed, j]])
                        {
                            t_size = i_fixed;
                            t_fish_id = map[i_fixed, j];
                            t_hard = pick_hard_quest;
                            quest_chosen = 1;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                i--;
            }
        }
    }
}

if (quest_chosen)
{
    t_count = 1;
    t_value = 1000;
    
    if (!exotic[t_size, t_fish_id])
    {
        if (t_size < 2)
        {
            t_count += 1;
            
            if (t_size < 1)
                t_count++;
        }
        else
        {
            t_value = 2000;
        }
    }
    else if (t_size >= 3)
    {
        t_value = 10000;
    }
    else
    {
        t_value = 5000;
    }
    
    if (t_hard)
        t_value *= 3;
}

if (!quest_chosen)
    all_quests_complete = 1;

            pop[2, 12] = min(pop[2, 12], 0);

        if (pop[0, 12] <= 0)

            pop[2, 14] = min(pop[2, 14], 1);
            pop[1, 18] = min(pop[1, 18], 1);
        }
        
        pop[0, 14] = min(pop[0, 14], pop[0, 15]);
        pop[0, 15] = pop[0, 14];
        
        if (pop[0, 2] <= 0)
            pop[1, 2] = 0;

            pop[0, 2] = 0;

            pop[2, 9] = 0;

            pop[2, 2] = 0;
        
        if (pop[2, 2] <= 0)
            pop[1, 4] = 0;
        
        if (pop[1, 14] <= 0)
            pop[0, 17] = 0;
        
        if (pop[0, 17] <= 0)

        if (pop[2, 29] <= 0)
            pop[1, 21] = 0;
        
        if (pop[1, 30] <= 0)
            pop[0, 34] = 0;
        
        if (pop[0, 34] <= 0)
            pop[1, 30] = 0;
        
        if (pop[0, 33] <= 0)
            pop[2, 31] = 0;
        
        if (pop[0, 24] <= 0)
            pop[3, 5] = 0;
        
        if (pop[0, 8] > 0)
            pop[0, 7] = max_pop[0, 7];
    }
    
    if (pop_loaded && max_pop_loaded)
    {
        if (!check_endangered)
        {
            check_endangered = 1;
            i = array_height_2d(object) - 1;
            
            while (i > -1)
            {
                j = array_length_2d(object, i) - 1;
                
                while (j > -1)
                {
                    extinct[i, j] = pop[i, j] <= 0;
                    j--;
                }
                
                i--;
            }
        }
    }
    else
    {
        check_endangered = 0;
    }
    
    counter++;
    
    if (pop_loaded && max_pop_loaded && (counter % 100) == 0)
    {
        i = array_height_2d(object) - 1;
        
        while (i > -1)
        {
            j = array_length_2d(object, i) - 1;
            
            while (j > -1)
            {
                if (!extinct[i, j])
                {
                    extinct[i, j] = pop[i, j] <= 0;
                    
                    if (extinct[i, j])
                    {
                        message = instance_create(0, 0, Message_Endangered);
                        message.text = name[i, j] + " are extinct.";
                    }
                }
                
                j--;
            }
            
            i--;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_CatSwim_Other_10.gml =====
while (!holding > 0)
{
    touch_x = x + (touch_x_unit * 10);
    touch_y = y + (touch_y_unit * 10);
    grab_radius = ctrl_timer * 10;
    grab_radius = clamp(grab_radius, 1, 10);
    touching = collision_circle(touch_x, touch_y, grab_radius, FishSmall, 1, 1);
    
    if (!touching && Girl.level >= 22)
        touching = collision_circle(touch_x, touch_y, grab_radius, FishMedium, 1, 1);
    
    if (touching > 0)
    {
        catswim_pickup(touching);
        break;
    }
    
    touching = collision_point(touch_x, touch_y, Gear, 1, 1);
    holding_gear = 0;
    
    if (touching > 0)
    {
        if (touching.state == 0)
        {
            holding_gear = 1;
            catswim_pickup_gear(touching);
            break;
        }
        else
        {
            touching = -1;
        }
    }
    
    for (attempts = 0; attempts < 20 && touching < 0; attempts++)
    {
        touch_x = x + (touch_x_unit * attempts * 10);
        touch_y = y + (touch_y_unit * attempts * 10);
        
        if (!collision_line(x, y, touch_x, touch_y, FishSmall, true, true))
            touching = collision_line(x, y, touch_x, touch_y, FishNotSmall, true, true);
    }
    
    if (touching > 0 && can_grab)
    {
        holding_big = 1;
        saved_speed = point_distance(0, 0, swim_x, swim_y);
        holding = touching;
        
        if (holding.object_index == Josie)
        {
            grabj = 1;
            holding.being_held = 1;
            
            if (!sent_message)
                instance_create(0, 0, Message_DontLetGo);
            
            sent_message = 1;
        }
        
        anchor = instance_create(touch_x, touch_y, CatAnchor);
        anchor.fish = holding;
        
        with (holding)
        {
            other.hold_x = fish_from_global_x(other.touch_x, other.touch_y);
            other.hold_y = fish_from_global_y(other.touch_x, other.touch_y);
        }
        
        hold_len = point_distance(0, 0, hold_x, hold_y);
        hold_dir = point_direction(0, 0, hold_x, hold_y);
        hold_angle_offset = 0;
        swim_x = 0;
        swim_y = 0;
        drift_x = 0;
        drift_y = 0;
        hold_flip = holding.image_yscale;
        hold_flip_angle = 0;
        
        if (hold_flip < 0)
            hold_flip_angle = -180;
        
        break;
    }
    
    break;

    if (other.isVisible)
    {
        nearest[0] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishMedium);
        nearest[1] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishLargeEarly);
        nearest[2] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishHuge);
        
        if ((distance_squared(x, y, nearest[1].x, nearest[1].y) + 40000) > distance_squared(x, y, nearest[2].x, nearest[2].y))
            nearest[1] = nearest[2];
        
        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        {
            square_distance = power(nearest[i].x - other.x, 2) + power(nearest[i].y - other.y, 2);
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min))
                square_distance = fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min;
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_cutoff * fish_distance_cutoff))
            {
                mag = forceScalar[i] / square_distance;
                dir = point_direction(nearest[i].x, nearest[i].y, other.x, other.y);
                other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
                other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
            }
        }
    }
    
    square_distance = power(other.start_x - other.x, 2) + power(other.start_y - other.y, 2);
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_max * home_distance_max))
        square_distance = home_distance_max * home_distance_max;
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_cutoff * home_distance_cutoff))
    {
        mag = forceScalar[2] * square_distance;
        dir = point_direction(other.x, other.y, other.start_x, other.start_y);
        other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
        other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
    }

        if (square_distance < (fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min))
            square_distance = fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min;
        
        if (square_distance < (fish_distance_cutoff * fish_distance_cutoff))
        {
            mag = forceScalar[i] / square_distance;
            dir = point_direction(nearest[i].x, nearest[i].y, other.x, other.y);
            other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
            other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
        }
    }
    
    square_distance = power(other.start_x - other.x, 2) + power(other.start_y - other.y, 2);
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_max * home_distance_max))
        square_distance = home_distance_max * home_distance_max;
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_cutoff * home_distance_cutoff))
    {
        mag = forceScalar[2] * square_distance;
        dir = point_direction(other.x, other.y, other.start_x, other.start_y);
        other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
        other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
    }
}

start_x += home_hspeed;

if (start_x > (TRUE_START_X + home_half_width))
    home_hspeed = -home_move_speed;

if (start_x < (TRUE_START_X - home_half_width))
    home_hspeed = home_move_speed;

hspeed *= 0.99;
vspeed *= 0.99;

if (speed > 10)
    speed = 10;

if (speed < 5)
    speed += 0.4;

if (y < 1294)
    vspeed += 1;

if (x < 1000)
    hspeed += 1;

    }

        {

forceScalar[0] = 4000;

home_distance_cutoff = 10;


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Cuddlefish_Other_10.gml =====
fish_lunge();

with (dataobject)
{
    if (other.isVisible)
    {
        nearest[0] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishMedium);
        nearest[1] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishLargeEarly);
        nearest[2] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishHuge);
        
        if ((distance_squared(x, y, nearest[1].x, nearest[1].y) + 40000) > distance_squared(x, y, nearest[2].x, nearest[2].y))
            nearest[1] = nearest[2];
        
        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        {
            square_distance = power(nearest[i].x - other.x, 2) + power(nearest[i].y - other.y, 2);
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min))
                square_distance = fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min;
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_cutoff * fish_distance_cutoff))
            {
                mag = forceScalar[i] / square_distance;
                dir = point_direction(nearest[i].x, nearest[i].y, other.x, other.y);
                other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
                other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
            }
        }
    }
    
    square_distance = power(other.start_x - other.x, 2) + power(other.start_y - other.y, 2);
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_max * home_distance_max))
        square_distance = home_distance_max * home_distance_max;
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_cutoff * home_distance_cutoff))
    {
        mag = forceScalar[2] * square_distance;
        dir = point_direction(other.x, other.y, other.start_x, other.start_y);
        other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
        other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
    }
}

hspeed *= 0.99;
vspeed *= 0.99;

if (speed > 10)
    speed = 10;

if (speed < 1)
    speed += 0.1;

if (y < 1294)
    vspeed += 1;

if (x < 1000)
    hspeed += 1;

    target_x += lengthdir_x(go_speed, dir_to_lure);
    target_y += lengthdir_y(go_speed, dir_to_lure);
    
    if (abs(x - target_x) > 3000)
        target_y = min(target_y, 1394);

    target_x += lengthdir_x(return_speed, dir_to_lure);
    target_y += lengthdir_y(return_speed, dir_to_lure);

        target_y = min(target_y, 1394);

    vspeed += (lengthdir_y(dist, dir) * 0.25);

            speed = 8;

    hspeed++;

i = array_height_2d(CatalogData.object) - 1;

while (i >= 0)
{
    fish_count[i] = 0;
    fish_total_health[i] = 0;
    k = array_length_2d(CatalogData.object, i) - 1;
    
    while (k >= 0)
    {
        j = CatalogData.map[i, k];
        
        if (!CatalogData.hide[i, j])
            fish_count[i]++;
        
        if (CatalogData.count[i, j] >= 0 || CatalogData.count[i, j] == -2)
        {
            item = instance_create(0, 0, CatalogItem);
            item.size = i;
            item.index = j;
            item.index_unmapped = k;
            item.fish = CatalogData.object[i, j];
            item.name = CatalogData.name[i, j];
            item.desc = CatalogData.desc[i, j];
            item.count = CatalogData.count[i, j];
            item.life = CatalogData.life[i, j];
            
            if (i == 3)
            {
                if (item.count < 0 && j == 10)
                    CatalogData.hide[i, j] = !CatalogData.showMagnalav;
                
                if (item.count < 0)
                    item.name = "???";
                
                if (j == 10 || j == 11)
                {
                    if (item.count == 0)
                        item.name = "???";
                }
            }
            
            if (CatalogData.count[i, j] == -2)
                item.desc = CatalogData.desc_hidden[i, j];
            
            if (CatalogData.count[i, j] == 0)
                item.desc = CatalogData.desc_seen[i, j];
            
            if (CatalogData.count[i, j] > 0)
                item.desc = CatalogData.desc_caught[i, j];
            
            if (CatalogData.pop[i, j] < (CatalogData.max_pop[i, j] / 2))
                item.desc = CatalogData.desc_n[i, j];
            
            if (CatalogData.pop[i, j] <= 0)
                item.desc = CatalogData.desc_e[i, j];
            
            if (!CatalogData.hide[i, j])
                fish_total_health[i] += item.life + 1;
        }
        
        k--;
    }
    
    fish_completion[i] = fish_total_health[i] * 0.25 * (1 / fish_count[i]);
    i--;
}

    fish_avoid_wall_stun(200, 60);

    record_safe = 1;
else
    record_safe = 0;

if (record_safe)
{
    if (!collision_point(safe_x, safe_y, Collidable, 1, 0))
    {
        long_safe_x = safe_x;
        long_safe_y = safe_y;
        record_safe = 0;
    }
}

{
    fish_update_target_coords();
    target_timer--;
    
    if (target_timer < 0 && y > 1294)
    {
        target_timer = TARGET_TIMER;
        fish_target_nearest_fish_late(target_index);
        
        if (target < 0)
            fish_target_nearest_fish(242);
        
        if (!allowCircling)
            fish_deloop();
    }
    
    fish_avoid_fish(242, avoid_large_range, avoid_large_force);
    
    if (target > 0)
        fish_elast_towards_target(0.025, 0.75);
    
    fish_fleehuge();

    safe_x += lengthdir_x(0.1, safe_direction);
    safe_y += lengthdir_y(0.1, safe_direction);

{
    if (speed > 5)
        speed += ((relax_speed - speed) * 0.02 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    
    if (y < min_y)
        vspeed += (v_correct * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    
    if (y > max_y)
        vspeed -= (v_correct * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    
    if (x < min_x)
        hspeed += (h_correct * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    
    if (y > max_x)
        hspeed -= (h_correct * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    
    if (x > 23000)
        hspeed -= (5 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    
    if (!deepfish && y > 3500)
        vspeed -= (5 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
}

    vspeed += 2;

        stamina += (0.222 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
        
        if (stamina > (MAX_STAMINA - fatigue))
            stamina = MAX_STAMINA - fatigue;

            isDragging = 1;

            {
                isDragging = 1;
                angle_velocity += (36 * ((irandom(1) * 2) - 1));
            }
            else
            {
                isDragging = 0;
            }
        }
        
        if (isDragging)
            target_angle += (90 + random(180));
    }
    else
    {
        if (Lure.wasJustReeling)
        {
            stamina -= (1 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
            fatigue += (0.5 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
        }
        else
        {
            stamina -= (0.1 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
        }

                if (stamina > 1.3)
                    stamina = 1.3;
            }
            else
            {
                isDragging = 0;
            }
        }
        
        if (stamina <= 0 || random(1) > (1 - ((Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar) / thrall_frames)))
            isDragging = 0;

    }

    image_angle += (angle_velocity * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    angle_velocity *= power(thrall_friction, Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar);
}
else if (Lure.fowled)

fish_lunge();

if (state != 1)
{
    with (data_object)
    {
        nearest_swarmer = instance_nearest(other.x + lengthdir_x(leader_distance, other.direction), other.y + lengthdir_y(leader_distance, other.direction), other.object_index);
        
        if (other.state == 0)
        {
            if (nearest_swarmer.id != other.id && nearest_swarmer.state != 6 && nearest_swarmer.state != 4)
            {
                other.start_x = nearest_swarmer.x + lengthdir_x(leader_lead, nearest_swarmer.direction);
                other.start_y = nearest_swarmer.y + lengthdir_y(leader_lead, nearest_swarmer.direction);
                home_force_multiplier = charm;
            }
            else
            {
                other.start_x = lengthdir_x(5000, other.direction + turn_angle);
                other.start_y = lengthdir_y(5000, other.direction + turn_angle);
                home_force_multiplier = turn_force;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            home_force_multiplier = return_force;
            
            if (point_distance(other.x, other.y, other.start_x, other.start_y) < start_point_distance_deadzone)
                state = 0;
        }
        
        if (other.isVisible)
        {
            nearest[0] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishMedium);
            nearest[1] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishLargeEarly);
            nearest[2] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishHuge);
            
            if ((distance_squared(x, y, nearest[1].x, nearest[1].y) + 40000) > distance_squared(x, y, nearest[2].x, nearest[2].y))
                nearest[1] = nearest[2];
            
            for (i = 0; i < 2; i++)
            {
                square_distance = power(nearest[i].x - other.x, 2) + power(nearest[i].y - other.y, 2);
                
                if (square_distance < (fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min))
                    square_distance = fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min;
                
                if (square_distance < (fish_distance_cutoff * fish_distance_cutoff))
                {
                    mag = forceScalar[i] / square_distance;
                    dir = point_direction(nearest[i].x, nearest[i].y, other.x, other.y);
                    other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
                    other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
                }
            }
        }
        
        square_distance = power(other.start_x - other.x, 2) + power(other.start_y - other.y, 2);
        
        if (square_distance > (home_distance_max * home_distance_max))
            square_distance = home_distance_max * home_distance_max;
        
        if (square_distance > (home_distance_cutoff * home_distance_cutoff))
        {
            mag = forceScalar[2] * square_distance * home_force_multiplier;
            dir = point_direction(other.x, other.y, other.start_x, other.start_y);
            other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
            other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
        }
    }
    
    hspeed *= 0.95;
    vspeed *= 0.95;
    
    if (speed > data_object.max_speed)
        speed = data_object.max_speed;
    
    if (speed < data_object.min_speed)
        speed += 0.25;
    
    if (y < data_object.minimum_depth)
        vspeed += 1;
    
    if (x < 1200)
        hspeed += 1;
    
    if ((((TRUE_START_X - x) * (TRUE_START_X - x)) + ((TRUE_START_Y - y) * (TRUE_START_Y - y))) > (data_object.wander_range * data_object.wander_range))
    {
        start_x = TRUE_START_X;
        start_y = TRUE_START_Y;
        state = 2;
    }
}

    with (random_fish(235))
    {
        if (object_is_ancestor(object_index, Bulbfish))
            continue;

            instance_deactivate_object(id);

        }
        
        other.attempts++;
    }

                if (start_x < 17000 && x < 17000 && state == 0)
                    state = 12;
                
                if (((start_x < 24000 && start_x > 17000) || (x < 24000 && x > 17000)) && state == 12)
                    state = 0;
                
                if (start_x > 24000 && x > 24000 && state == 0)
                    state = 12;
            }
        }
        else if (active_x < 26000 && zone_enabled != 6)
        {
            zone_enabled = 6;
            
            with (Fish)
            {
                if (always_zoned)
                    continue;
                
                if (start_x < 20000 && x < 20000 && state == 0)
                    state = 12;
                
                if (((start_x < 29500 && start_x > 20000) || (x < 29500 && x > 20000)) && state == 12)
                    state = 0;
                
                if (start_x > 29500 && x > 29500 && state == 0)
                    state = 12;
            }
        }
        else if (active_x > 26000 && zone_enabled != 7)
        {
            zone_enabled = 7;
            
            with (Fish)
            {
                if (always_zoned)
                    continue;
                
                if (start_x < 19000 && x < 19000 && state == 0)
                    state = 12;
                
                if (start_x > 25000 || (x > 25000 && state == 12))
                    state = 0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    if (fps < 30)
        cull_trigger += 5;
    else if (fps < 45)
        cull_trigger += 1;
    else if (fps < 60)
        cull_trigger += 0.5;
    else
        cull_trigger = 0;
    
    just_culled = 0;
    
    if (cull_trigger > 120)
    {
        cull_trigger = 0;
        just_culled = 1;
        event_perform(ev_other, ev_user0);
    }
}

    temp_weight[max_id] = -100;
    map[i] = max_id;
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_GearData_Other_10.gml =====
null_weight = 0;

for (i = 0; i < n_gear; i++)
{
    spawn = 1;
    
    if (unlocked[i])
        reduceRate = 1;
    else
        reduceRate = 0;
    
    if (spawn)
    {
        with (Gear)
        {
            if (object_index == other.gear[other.i])
                other.spawn = 0;
        }
    }
    
    if (spawn)
    {
        if (gear[i] == 476)
        {
            if (is_it_night())
                true_weight[i] = 8;
            else if (Girl.level < 16)
                true_weight[i] = 0;
            else
                true_weight[i] = weight[i];
        }
        else
        {
            true_weight[i] = weight[i];
        }
    }
    else
    {
        null_weight += 0.2;
        true_weight[i] = 0;
    }
    
    if (reduceRate)
        true_weight[i] *= 0.25;
}

if (LureData.unlocked[2] <= 0)
    true_weight[19] = 0;

if ((time_last_spawn + SPAWN_DELAY) < current_time)
{
    time_last_spawn = current_time;
    sum = null_weight;
    
    for (i = 0; i < n_gear; i++)
        sum += true_weight[i];
    
    if (sum > 0)
    {
        repeat (2)
        {
            roll = random(sum) + 0.00001;
            
            if (roll > null_weight)
            {
                sum = 0;
                
                for (i = 0; i < n_gear; i++)
                {
                    sum += true_weight[i];
                    
                    if (roll < sum)
                        break;
                }
                
                if (i < n_gear)
                    gear_spawn(i);
            }
        }
    }
}

{
    item = instance_create(0, 0, HatalogItem);
    item.index = Girl.gear_id;
    item.value = GearData.value[Girl.gear_id];
    item.gear = GearData.gear[Girl.gear_id];
    item.weight = GearData.weight[Girl.gear_id];
    item.equipped = 1;
    item.desc = Girl.gear.desc;
    item.sprite = Girl.gear.sprite_index;
    item.name = Girl.gear.name;
    item.offset_x = GearData.offset_x[Girl.gear_id];
    item.offset_y = GearData.offset_y[Girl.gear_id];
    item.image_xscale = surfw;
    hats[hats_found] = item;
    hats_found++;
}

for (j = 0; j < GearData.n_gear; j++)
{
    i = GearData.map[j];
    
    if (GearData.unlocked[i] && Girl.gear_id != i)
    {
        item = instance_create(0, 0, HatalogItem);
        item.index = i;
        item.value = GearData.value[i];
        item.gear = GearData.gear[i];
        item.weight = GearData.weight[i];
        item.equipped = 0;
        item.offset_x = GearData.offset_x[i];
        item.offset_y = GearData.offset_y[i];
        temporary_gear = instance_create(-500, -500, item.gear);
        item.desc = temporary_gear.desc;
        item.sprite = temporary_gear.sprite_index;
        item.name = temporary_gear.name;
        
        with (temporary_gear)
            instance_destroy();
        
        item.image_xscale = surfw;
        hats[hats_found] = item;
        hats_found++;
    }
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_icon_Reset_Other_10.gml =====
action_inherited();

if (doNothing)
{
}
else
{
    with (CatalogData)
    {
        i = array_height_2d(object) - 1;
        
        while (i > -1)
        {
            j = array_length_2d(object, i) - 1;
            
            while (j > -1)
            {
                weight[i, j] = 1;
                life[i, j] = -1;
                count[i, j] = min(count[i, j], 0);
                j--;
            }
            
            i--;
        }
        
        i = array_height_2d(object) - 1;
        
        while (i > -1)
        {
            j = array_length_2d(object, i) - 1;
            
            while (j > -1)
            {
                pop[i, j] = max_pop[i, j];
                j--;
            }
            
            i--;
        }
    }
    
    with (Handler)
        instance_destroy();
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_LensCap_Draw_0.gml =====
draw_self();

if (state == 2)
{
    set[0] = 236;
    set[1] = 240;
    set[2] = 242;
    
    for (i = 0; i < 3; i++)
    {
        dist_sqr = 0;
        draw_set_circle_precision(12 + (i * 4));
        
        with (set[i])
        {
            other.circle_radius = 0;
            other.dist_sqr = ((x - Camera.x) * (x - Camera.x)) + ((y - Camera.y) * (y - Camera.y));
            
            if (state == 0 || state == 2 || state == 9)
            {
                other.circle_radius = (Lure.lunge_scale * LUNGE_RANGE_START * base_lunge_mod) + base_lunge_offset;
                other.color = 8421504;
            }
            else if (state == 1)
            {
                other.circle_radius = (LUNGE_RANGE_STOP * base_lunge_mod) + base_lunge_offset;
                other.color = 255;
            }
            
            if (other.circle_radius > 0)
            {
                if (other.dist_sqr < ((600 + other.circle_radius) * (600 + other.circle_radius)))
                {
                    if (other.circle_radius > 500)
                        draw_set_circle_precision(32);
                    
                    draw_set_color(other.color);
                    draw_circle(x, y, other.circle_radius, 1);
                    
                    if (other.circle_radius > 500)
                        draw_set_circle_precision(12 + (other.i * 4));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

if (!surface_exists(light_surface))
    light_surface = surface_create(width, height);

if (!surface_exists(mini_surface))
    mini_surface = surface_create(100, 100);

if (view_current == 0)
{
    surface_set_target(light_surface);
    draw_clear(c_black);
    top_color = color_from_depth(Camera.y - (height * 0.5));
    bot_color = color_from_depth(Camera.y + (height * 0.5));
    draw_rectangle_colour(0, 0, width, height, top_color, top_color, bot_color, bot_color, 0);
    draw_set_blend_mode(bm_subtract);
    
    with (AbstractLight)
    {
        if (visible)
        {
            x_original = x;
            y_original = y;
            x = x_original - view_xview[0];
            y = y_original - view_yview[0];
            draw_self();
            x = x_original;
            y = y_original;
        }
    }
    
    draw_set_blend_mode(bm_normal);
    draw_set_alpha(lightning_flash);
    draw_set_color(c_black);
    draw_rectangle(0, 0, width, height, false);
    draw_set_alpha(1);
    surface_reset_target();
}
else if (view_current == 1)
{
    surface_set_target(mini_surface);
    draw_clear(c_black);
    top_color = color_from_depth(view_yview[1]);
    bot_color = color_from_depth(view_yview[1] + view_hview[1]);
    draw_rectangle_colour(0, 0, 100, 100, top_color, top_color, bot_color, bot_color, 0);
    draw_set_blend_mode(bm_subtract);
    
    with (AbstractLight)
    {
        if (visible)
        {
            x_original = x;
            y_original = y;
            x = (x_original - view_xview[1]) * 0.1666;
            y = (y_original - view_yview[1]) * 0.1666;
            image_xscale *= 0.1666;
            image_yscale *= 0.1666;
            draw_self();
            x = x_original;
            y = y_original;
            image_xscale /= 0.1666;
            image_yscale /= 0.1666;
        }
    }
    
    draw_set_blend_mode(bm_normal);
    draw_set_alpha(lightning_flash);
    draw_set_color(c_black);
    draw_rectangle(0, 0, 100, 100, false);
    draw_set_alpha(1);
    surface_reset_target();
}

if (view_current == 0)
{
    draw_set_blend_mode(bm_subtract);
    draw_surface(light_surface, view_xview[0], view_yview[0]);
    draw_set_blend_mode(bm_normal);
}
else if (view_current == 1)
{
    draw_set_blend_mode(bm_subtract);
    draw_surface_stretched(mini_surface, view_xview[1], view_yview[1], view_wview[1], view_hview[1]);
    draw_set_blend_mode(bm_normal);
}

if (view_current == 0 || view_current == 1)
{
    if (lightning_flash > 0.3)
    {
        draw_set_blend_mode(bm_add);
        
        if (Girl.torby_are_extinct && Lure.state == 0 && Rod.state == 2)
        {
            if (lightning_flash > 0.5)
            {
                ox = -100 + (10 * power(random(1), 3) * sign(random(1) - 0.5));
                oy = -100 + (10 * power(random(1), 3) * sign(random(1) - 0.5));
                draw_sprite_ext(Torby.sprite_index, 0, Girl.x + ox, Girl.y + oy, -1, 1, 0, c_white, 0.3);
            }
            
            ghost_is_visible = 1;
        }
        else
        {
            ghost_is_visible = 0;
        }
        
        draw_set_alpha(clamp(lightning_flash * 0.5, 0, 1));
        draw_set_color(lightning_color);
        draw_rectangle(view_xview[view_current], view_yview[view_current], view_xview[view_current] + view_wview[view_current], view_yview[view_current] + view_hview[view_current], false);
        draw_set_blend_mode(bm_normal);
    }
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_LightingSystem_Step_0.gml =====
if (is_it_storming())
{
    chance = 0.9982;
    
    if (is_it_halloween())
        chance = 0.995;
    
    if (!primed && random(1) > chance)
    {
        roll = random(1);
        total = 0;
        
        for (i = 0; i < n_thunds; i++)
        {
            total += thund_weight[i];
            
            if (roll < total)
                break;
        }
        
        thund_index = min(i, n_thunds);
        magnitude = thund_rand[thund_index] + thund_base[thund_index];
        LightingSystem.lightning = magnitude;
        delay_timer = thund_delay[thund_index];
        primed = 1;
    }
}

if (primed)
{
    delay_timer--;
    
    if (delay_timer < 0)
    {
        thunder_sound = audio_play_sound(thund_sound[thund_index], 0, false);
        audio_sound_gain(thunder_sound, magnitude / 3, 0);
        delay_timer = 0;
        primed = 0;
    }
}

lightning_flash = min(1, (random(lightning) * 0.5) + (lightning_flash * 0.8));
lightning -= 0.04;

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Lure_Keyboard_17.gml =====
if (!ctrl_pressed || (instance_exists(CatSwim) && CatSwim.canFlick))
{
    ctrl_pressed = 1;
    var flick_reasonable = !instance_exists(CatSwim) || CatSwim.canFlick;
    debug_enabled = 0;
    
    if (debug_enabled)
    {
        Lure.x = mouse_x;
        Lure.y = mouse_y;
    }
    else if (Rod.upgrades[21] && Lure.state == 2 && flick_reasonable)
    {
        rise_speed = 0;
        sinker_vel = 0;
        var flick_target_x = mouse_x;
        var flick_target_y = mouse_y;
        
        if (instance_exists(CatSwim))
        {
            max_flick = min(max_flick + 10, 200);
            flick_target_x = CatSwim.x;
            flick_target_y = CatSwim.y;
            CatSwim.has_lure = 1;
        }
        
        if (Lure.hookedFishSize > 0)
        {
            flick_stuck = 0;
            
            if (Lure.fish.object_index == Conpas.object_index)
                flick_stuck = 1;
            
            if (Lure.fish.object_index == Smarty.object_index)
                flick_stuck = 1;
            
            if (Lure.fish.object_index == Puff.object_index)
                flick_stuck = 1;
            
            if (Lure.fish.object_index == Boom_Puff.object_index)
                flick_stuck = 1;
            
            if (Worm.state == 3)
                flick_stuck = 1;
            
            if (((Lure.fish.isDragging && random(1) < 0.25) || Girl.gear_finicky) && fish.life >= fish.MAX_LIFE && !flick_stuck)
            {
                Lure.fish.lungeCooldown = 60;
                lure_unhook();
            }
            else
            {
                Lure.fish.thrall_override = 100;
                
                if (Lure.fish.object_index != Conpas.object_index)
                    Lure.fish.thrall_override = 10;
                
                Lure.fish.thrall_override_angle = point_direction(x, y, flick_target_x, flick_target_y);
            }
        }
        
        if (Lure.touching_rock)
        {
            dir_to_mouse = point_direction(Lure.x, Lure.y, flick_target_x, flick_target_y);
            dis_to_mouse = point_distance(Lure.x, Lure.y, flick_target_x, flick_target_y);
            dis_to_mouse = min(dis_to_mouse, max_flick);
            tx = Lure.x + lengthdir_x(dis_to_mouse, dir_to_mouse);
            ty = Lure.y + lengthdir_y(dis_to_mouse, dir_to_mouse);
            var attempts = 0;
            var d2l = point_direction(tx, ty, Lure.x, Lure.y);
            var r2l = 1;
            var dist2l = point_distance(tx, ty, Lure.x, Lure.y);
            
            while ((attempts * r2l) < dist2l && collision_point(tx, ty, Collidable, 1, 0))
            {
                attempts++;
                tx += lengthdir_x(1 * r2l, d2l);
                ty += lengthdir_y(1 * r2l, d2l);
            }
            
            var dir = point_direction(x, y, tx, ty);
            padded_x = x + lengthdir_x(30, dir);
            padded_y = y + lengthdir_y(30, dir);
            
            if (!collision_line(padded_x, padded_y, tx, ty, Collidable, true, false))
            {
                Lure.x = tx;
                Lure.y = ty;
                break_chance = 0.12;
                
                if (Lure.hookedFishSize == 0)
                    break_chance = 0.05;
                
                if (Lure.size != -2)
                {
                    if (random(1) < 0.12)
                    {
                        with (Lure)
                            line_snap();
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        Lure.fowled = 0;
                        Lure.tether_tension = 0;
                        Lure.sinker_vel = 0;
                        Lure.rise_speed = 1;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        else
        {
            Lure.justFlicked = 1;
            var dir_temp = point_direction(x, y, flick_target_x, flick_target_y);
            var dist_temp = point_distance(x, y, flick_target_x, flick_target_y);
            newx = Lure.x + lengthdir_x(min(dist_temp, max_flick), dir_temp);
            newy = Lure.y + lengthdir_y(min(dist_temp, max_flick), dir_temp);
            
            if (!collision_line(x, y, newx, newy, Collidable, true, false))
            {
                Lure.x = newx;
                Lure.y = newy;
            }
            
            max_flick = max(0, max_flick - 50);
        }
    }
}

if (!instance_exists(Rocket) || Rocket.fuel <= 0)

    reel_button_activated = 1;

tether_x = Rod.x + Rod.hspeed + lengthdir_x(ROD_LENGTH, Rod.angle + ROD_ANGLE);
tether_y = Rod.y + Rod.vspeed + lengthdir_y(ROD_LENGTH, Rod.angle + ROD_ANGLE);
tether_distance = point_distance(tether_x, tether_y, x, y);
tether_direction = point_direction(tether_x, tether_y, x, y);

if (state != 3)
{
    if (state != 1 || !collision_circle(x, y, 30, Boat, 1, 1))
    {
        if (y > 1244)
        {
            in_the_water = 1;
            near_the_water = 1;
            
            if (state == 1)
            {
                if (Rod.upgrades[15])
                    instance_create(x, y, CastShield);
                
                tether_length = point_distance(tether_x, tether_y, x, y);
                cast_distance = x - tether_x;
                state = 2;
                speed = 0;
                
                if (Lure.hookedFishSize > 0)
                {
                    if (Lure.hookedFishSize < 3)
                    {
                        if (Lure.fish.object_index != Bombat)
                        {
                            if (Worm.state != 3)
                                lure_fish_to_bait();
                        }
                        else
                        {
                        }
                    }
                    else

                        lure_fish_go_free();
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        else if (y > 1204)
        {
            in_the_water = 0;
            near_the_water = 1;

            {
                wasJustReeling = 0;
                playedSplash = 1;
                audio_play_sound(sfx_lure_splash, 0, false);
            }
        }
        else
        {
            in_the_water = 0;
            near_the_water = 0;
        }
    }
    
    if (state != 2)
        wasJustReeling = 0;
    
    switch (state)
    {
        case 0:
            Lure.canBite = 1;
            tether_tension = 0;
            locked = 0;
            sharkPulls = 0;
            audio_stop_sound(sfx_reel_loop);
            audio_stop_sound(sfx_reel_loop_out);
            playedSplash = 0;
            showReleasedTooSoon = 1;
            
            if (tether_distance > TETHER_SLACK)
            {
                x = tether_x + lengthdir_x(TETHER_SLACK, tether_direction);
                y = tether_y + lengthdir_y(TETHER_SLACK, tether_direction);
                delta_x = x - x_previous;
                delta_y = y - y_previous;
                speed = (0.95 * point_distance(0, 0, delta_x, delta_y)) / (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar);
            }
            
            delta_x = x - x_previous;
            delta_y = y - y_previous;
            direction = point_direction(0, 0, delta_x, delta_y);
            break;
        
        case 1:
            audio_stop_sound(sfx_reel_loop);
            rise_speed = 0;
            break;
        
        case 2:
            audio_stop_sound(sfx_reel_loop_out);
            
            if (forced_reel_enabled && Rod.upgrades[2] == 0 && !hasEquipment)
            {
                if (hookedFishSize > 0 && fish.life <= 0)
                    forced_reel = 1;
                else if (size <= 0)
                    forced_reel = 1;
                else
                    forced_reel = 0;
            }
            else
            {
                forced_reel = 0;
            }
            
            if (reel_button_activated || tether_length < 100 || forced_reel)
            {
                sinker_vel = 0;
                
                if (!wasJustReeling)
                {
                    reel_events++;
                    wasJustReeling = 1;
                    reel_sound = audio_play_sound(sfx_reel_loop, 0, true);
                    reel_pitch = 1;
                }
                
                if (hookedFishSize > 0 && fish.life == 3)
                {
                    if (fish.isDragging)
                        tether_tension_mod = 1 + (fish.size * abs(angle_difference(fish.image_angle, tether_direction) / 180));
                    else
                        tether_tension_mod = 0;
                }
                else
                {
                    tether_tension_mod = 0;
                }
                
                MAX_TENSION = 240 + (100 * Rod.rod_power) + (120 * Rod.upgrades[13]);
                
                if (Girl.RealismMode && !instance_exists(PotEffect_Red))
                    tether_tension += (5 * tether_tension_mod * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
                
                if (tether_length < 100)
                    tether_tension = min(tether_tension, MAX_TENSION - 50);
                
                if (tether_tension > MAX_TENSION && tether_length > 100)
                    line_snap();
                
                reel_pitch = 1 + (0.25 * power(min(tether_tension, MAX_TENSION) / MAX_TENSION, 8));
                audio_sound_pitch(reel_sound, reel_pitch);
                
                if (in_the_water)
                    tether_length = point_distance(tether_x, tether_y, x, y);
                
                modified_reel_speed = (Rod.reel_speed - (min(hookedFishSize, 3) * 2)) + Rod.upgrades[1];
                
                if (size <= 0 && Rod.upgrades[14])
                    modified_reel_speed = clamp(tether_length * 0.025, modified_reel_speed + 5, 50);
                
                modified_reel_speed = max(5, modified_reel_speed);
                
                if (instance_exists(PotEffect_Blue))
                    modified_reel_speed = max(30, modified_reel_speed);
                
                tether_length -= (modified_reel_speed * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
                
                if (tether_distance < TETHER_SLACK)
                {
                    cast_complete();
                    
                    if (Lure.size <= 0)
                        Lure.size = Girl.gear_bait_size;
                    
                    state = 0;
                    Rod.state = 0;
                }
                
                if (tether_distance > tether_length)
                {
                    x = tether_x + lengthdir_x(tether_length, tether_direction);
                    y = tether_y + lengthdir_y(tether_length, tether_direction);
                    
                    if (!in_the_water)
                        hspeed *= 0.8;
                }
                
                if (in_the_water && hookedFishSize <= 0 && Rod.upgrades[17] > 0 && y > 1299)
                {
                    rise_speed = RISE_SPEED + Rod.upgrades[17] + SINK_SPEED + Rod.upgrades[0];
                    
                    if (x > tether_x)
                    {
                        x += lengthdir_x(RISE_SPEED + Rod.upgrades[17], tether_direction + 90);
                        y += lengthdir_y(RISE_SPEED + Rod.upgrades[17], tether_direction + 90);
                        
                        if (x < tether_x)
                            x = tether_x;
                    }
                    else if (x < tether_x)
                    {
                        x += lengthdir_x(RISE_SPEED + Rod.upgrades[17], tether_direction - 90);
                        y += lengthdir_y(RISE_SPEED + Rod.upgrades[17], tether_direction - 90);
                        
                        if (x > tether_x)
                            x = tether_x;
                    }
                }
            }
            else
            {
                wasJustReeling = 0;
                audio_stop_sound(sfx_reel_loop);
                
                if (Lure.recover_tension)
                    tether_tension = (tether_tension - (5 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar))) * power(0.95, Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar);
                
                if (tether_tension < 0)
                    tether_tension = 0;
                
                rise_speed *= power(0.935 + (Rod.upgrades[17] * 0.005), Girl.delta_fixed);
                
                if (in_the_water)
                {
                    var sink_rate = ((SINK_SPEED - rise_speed) + Rod.upgrades[0]) * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar);
                    
                    if (x > tether_x)
                    {
                        x += lengthdir_x(sink_rate / Girl.delta_fixed, tether_direction - 90);
                        y += lengthdir_y(sink_rate / Girl.delta_fixed, tether_direction - 90);
                        
                        if (x < tether_x)
                            x = tether_x;
                    }
                    else if (x < tether_x)
                    {
                        x += lengthdir_x(sink_rate / Girl.delta_fixed, tether_direction + 90);
                        y += lengthdir_y(sink_rate / Girl.delta_fixed, tether_direction + 90);
                        
                        if (x > tether_x)
                            x = tether_x;
                    }
                    
                    if (Rod.upgrades[20] && abs(angle_difference(tether_direction, -90)) < 5 && size >= 0 && !collision_point(x, y, CaveRock, 1, 1) && (Lure.hookedFishSize == 0 || !Lure.fish.isDragging) && tether_distance < (Girl.gear_sinker_mod + Rod.sinker_distance))
                    {
                        y += ((5 + sinker_vel) * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
                        
                        if (sinker_vel < 5)
                            sinker_vel += ((0.05 + (sinker_vel * 0.01)) * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
                        else
                            sinker_vel = 5;
                    }
                }
                else if (rise_speed > (SINK_SPEED + Rod.upgrades[0]))
                {
                    rise_speed = SINK_SPEED + Rod.upgrades[0];
                }
                
                tether_length = point_distance(tether_x, tether_y, x, y);
            }
            
            break;
        
        case 3:
            audio_stop_sound(sfx_reel_loop);
            
            if (!audio_is_playing(sfx_reel_loop_out))
                audio_play_sound(sfx_reel_loop_out, 0, true);
            
            break;
    }
    
    if (!in_the_water)
        vspeed += (LURE_GRAVITY * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
    else
        speed = 0;
    
    if (state == 1)
    {
        if (reel_button_activated && Rod.upgrades[19])
        {
            audio_stop_sound(sfx_reel_loop_out);
            
            if (!locked)
            {
                lock_distance = tether_distance;
                
                if (lock_distance < 5)
                    lock_distance = 5;
                
                locked = 1;
                saved_hspeed = hspeed * 0.5;
                saved_vspeed = vspeed * 0.5;
                hspeed -= saved_hspeed;
                vspeed -= saved_vspeed;
                x_previous2 = x;
                y_previous2 = y;
            }
        }
        
        if (locked)
        {
            if (tether_distance > lock_distance)
            {
                x = tether_x + lengthdir_x(lock_distance, tether_direction);
                y = tether_y + lengthdir_y(lock_distance, tether_direction);
                delta_x = x - x_previous2;
                delta_y = y - y_previous2;
                dist = point_distance(0, 0, delta_x, delta_y);
                dir = point_direction(0, 0, delta_x, delta_y);
                
                if (!near_the_water)
                {
                    hspeed = lengthdir_x(dist, dir) / (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar);
                    vspeed = lengthdir_y(dist, dir) / (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar);
                }
                else
                {
                    vspeed *= power(0.5, Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar);
                    hspeed *= power(0.5, Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar);
                }
                
                if (speed > ((Rod.CAST_SPEED - 6) * 8))
                    speed = (Rod.CAST_SPEED - 6) * 8;
                
                x_previous2 = x;
                y_previous2 = y;
            }
        }
        else
        {
        }
        
        if (!reel_button_activated)
        {
            if (!audio_is_playing(sfx_reel_loop_out))
                audio_play_sound(sfx_reel_loop_out, 0, true);
            
            if (locked)
            {
                locked = 0;
                hspeed += (saved_hspeed * 1.2);
                vspeed += (saved_vspeed * 1.2);
                saved_hspeed = 0;
                saved_vspeed = 0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    x_previous = x;
    y_previous = y;
}

if (Rod.sparks > 0 && Lure.size > 0 && Lure.hookedFishSize <= 0)
{
    if (random(1) < (1 / (18 - (3 * Rod.sparks))))
    {
        glitter_x = x + lengthdir_x(18, image_angle - 90);
        glitter_y = y + lengthdir_y(18, image_angle - 90);

    move_target_timer = WANDER_DELAY;
    target_x = (random(wander_range) - (wander_range * 0.5)) + start_x;

    thrall_friction = 0.9;

    {

            {
                image_yscale *= -1;
                image_angle += 180;

        target_angle = point_direction(x, y, Lure.tether_x, Lure.tether_y) + 180;

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Maw_Other_10.gml =====
if (target > 0 && instance_exists(target) && target.state != 6 && !target.onBoat && (target.state != 3 || Lure.state == 2))
{
    target_attempts = 0;
    hspeed += ((target.x - x) * 0.005);
    vspeed += ((target.y - y) * 0.005);
    speed = (speed * 0.9) + 0.1;
    
    if (point_distance(x, y, target.x, target.y) < 20)
    {
        if (target.state == 3)
        {
            if (instance_exists(BigHook))
            {
                Lure.fish = id;
                Lure.hookedFishSize = size;
                state = 3;
                goTowardsBoat = 1;
                diveOutOfWater = 1;
            }
            else
            {
                instance_create(0, 0, Message_NeedBigHook);
                Lure.hookedFishSize = 0;
                Lure.size = -1;
                Lure.fish = -1;
                Lure.stolenBy = id;
                state = 5;
                alarm[0] = NOTHUNGRY_TIMER;
            }
        }
        
        with (target)
        {
            audio_play_sound(sfx_mawpop, 0, false);
            fish_die();
        }
        
        target = -1;
    }
}
else
{
    speed *= 0.95;
    var max_attempts = 311;
    
    if (target_attempts > max_attempts)
    {
        target = -1;
        target_attempts = 0;
    }
    else
    {
        var scalar = target_attempts / max_attempts;
        var angle = scalar * 6871;
        var dist = scalar * 30000;
        target = instance_nearest(x + lengthdir_x(200 + dist, direction + angle), y + lengthdir_y(200 + dist, direction + angle), Bulbfish);
        
        if (target > 0)
        {
            if (target.object_index == Bulbfish)
            {
                if (target.state == 7)
                    target = -1;
            }
        }
    }
    
    target_attempts++;
}

fish_lunge();
depth = -18.15;


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Maw_Other_12.gml =====

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Maw_Other_13.gml =====
stamina = MAX_STAMINA - fatigue;

if ((fatigue / MAX_STAMINA) < 0.5)
{
    isDragging = 1;
    doveOut = 0;
    
    if (goTowardsBoat)
    {
        Lure.tether_tension = 0;
        target_angle = point_direction(x, y, Lure.tether_x, Lure.tether_y) - 20;
        thrall_force = (y / room_height) * 30;
        
        if (point_distance(x, y, Lure.tether_x, Lure.tether_y) < 1200)
            goTowardsBoat = 0;
    }
    else if (y < 4100)
    {
        thrall_ang_force = 0.017;
        thrall_friction = 0.9;
        thrall_force = 15;
        target_angle = -90;
        dark_timer = 150;
    }
    else
    {
        dark_timer--;
        
        if ((Lure.tether_x + 2000) < x)
            target_angle = 170;
        
        if ((Lure.tether_x + 1000) > x)
            target_angle = 10;
        
        if (dark_timer <= 0)
            goTowardsBoat = 1;
    }
}
else if ((fatigue / MAX_STAMINA) < 0.8)
{
    isDragging = 1;
    thrall_force = 30;
    thrall_ang_force = 0.017;
    thrall_friction = 0.9;
    
    if (diveOutOfWater)
    {
        if (y > 1194 && !doveOut)
        {
            target_angle = 70;
        }
        else
        {
            doveOut = 1;
            
            if (target_angle > -90)
            {
                target_angle -= 10;
            }
            else
            {
                target_angle = -90;
                
                if (y > (room_height - 2000))
                    diveOutOfWater = 0;
            }
        }
    }
    else
    {
        target_angle += 5;
        thrall_force = (stamina / MAX_STAMINA) * 50;
    }
}
else
{
    thrall_ang_force = 0.025;
    thrall_friction = 0.6;
    
    if (y > 1794)
    {
        isDragging = 0;
    }
    else if (y < 1594)
    {
        if (point_distance(Lure.x, Lure.y, Lure.tether_x, Lure.tether_y) > 100 && Lure.state == 2)
        {
            if (!isDragging)
            {
                isDragging = 1;
                
                if (stamina > 1)
                    angle_velocity -= 10;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            isDragging = 0;
            
            if (abs(angle_difference(image_angle + 180, target_angle)) > 15)
            {
                thrall_ang_force = 0.017;
                thrall_friction = 0.9;
            }
            else
            {
                thrall_ang_force = 0.003;
                thrall_friction = 0.1;
            }
        }
    }
    
    target_angle = point_direction(Girl.x, Girl.y, x, y);
    var height_scalar = clamp(1 - ((y - 1294) / 300), 0, 1);
    var angle_scalar = clamp(1 - (abs(angle_difference(image_angle + 180, target_angle)) / 180), 0, 1);
    var stamina_scalar = clamp(stamina / MAX_STAMINA / 0.2, 0, 1);
    thrall_force = 3 + (50 * height_scalar * stamina_scalar * angle_scalar);
}

action_inherited();

    x += ((Camera.x - x) * 0.3);
    
    if ((Camera.x - x) < 10)
    {
        headingIn = 0;
        counter = DISPLAY_DURATION;
    }
}
else if (headingOut)
{
    hspeed += 5;
    
    if (x > (Camera.x + X_BUFFER))
        instance_destroy();
}
else
{
    if (counter > 0)
    {
        counter -= Girl.delta_fixed;
        
        if (counter <= 0)
        {
            counter = 0;
            alarm[0] = 1;
        }
    }
    
    x = Camera.x;
}

y = view_yview[0] + (view_hview[0] * 0.5);

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_NavBrain_Other_4.gml =====
var edgeList = ds_list_create();
var connectionsMap = ds_map_create();

with (NavNode)
{
    var current = id;
    
    with (NavNode)
    {
        if (current != id)
        {
            var col = collision_line(current.x, current.y, x, y, CaveRock, true, false);
            
            if (!col)
            {
                var connectionKey = string(current) + "_" + string(id);
                
                if (!ds_map_exists(connectionsMap, connectionKey))
                {
                    var newEdge = instance_create(current.x, current.y, NavEdge);
                    newEdge.startNode = current;
                    newEdge.endNode = id;
                    ds_list_add(current.edges, newEdge);
                    ds_list_add(edges, newEdge);
                    ds_map_add(connectionsMap, connectionKey, 1);
                    ds_list_add(edgeList, newEdge);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

for (var i = 0; i < ds_list_size(edgeList); i++)
    var navEdge = ds_list_find_value(edgeList, i);

ds_list_destroy(edgeList);
ds_map_destroy(connectionsMap);

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_NetCenter_Draw_0.gml =====
draw_set_color(c_white);
dir = 0;
n_lines = 5;

with (NetWeight)
{
    dir = other.dir;
    
    for (i = 0; i < other.n_lines; i++)
    {
        dx[i, 0] = other.x;
        dy[i, 0] = other.y;
        dir = (360 / other.n_lines) * i;
        
        for (j = 1; j < 7; j++)
        {
            dx2 = dx[i, j - 1] + lengthdir_x(20 * i, dir);
            dy2 = dy[i, j - 1] + lengthdir_y(20 * i, dir);
            
            if (j >= 5)
            {
                dx2 = x;
                dy2 = y;
            }
            else
            {
                dx2 += ((x - dx2) * 0.8 * (1 - (1 / j)));
                dy2 += ((y - dy2) * 0.8 * (1 - (1 / j)));
            }
            
            draw_line(dx[i, j - 1], dy[i, j - 1], dx2, dy2);
            dx[i, j] = dx2;
            dy[i, j] = dy2;
        }
    }
    
    for (j = 2; j < 7; j += 2)
    {
        for (i = 0; i < (other.n_lines - 1); i++)
            draw_line(dx[i, j], dy[i, j], dx[i + 1, j], dy[i + 1, j]);
        
        draw_line(dx[other.n_lines - 1, j], dy[other.n_lines - 1, j], dx[0, j], dy[0, j]);
    }
    
    dir += 180;
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_NodeSeraph_Other_13.gml =====
if (!isDoneClimbing)
{
    isReturning = 0;
    target_angle += (0.2 * thrall_force);
    thrall_force += 1;
    
    if (thrall_force > (20 + (10 * (stamina / MAX_STAMINA))))
        thrall_force = 20 + (10 * (stamina / MAX_STAMINA));
    
    if (target_angle > 70)
        target_angle = 70;
    
    if (y < 1294)
        isDoneClimbing = 1;
}
else if (!isReturning)
{
    target_angle -= 8;
    
    if (y > 1294)
    {
        thrall_force -= 1;
        
        if (thrall_force < (10 + (5 * power(stamina / MAX_STAMINA, 0.25))))
            thrall_force = 10 + (5 * power(stamina / MAX_STAMINA, 0.25));
    }
    
    if (y > ((1694 + MAX_STAMINA) - stamina))
        isDoneClimbing = 0;
    
    if (x > (start_x + 1000))
        isReturning = 1;
}
else
{
    stamina = MAX_STAMINA;
    fatigue = 0;
    target_angle = point_direction(x, y, Girl.x, Girl.y);
    
    if (x < start_x)
        isReturning = 0;
}

action_inherited();

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_ParallaxBackground_Draw_0.gml =====
draw_rectangle_colour(0, 5000, room_width, room_height, c_black, c_black, c_black, c_black, 0);
dy = max(6000, 7000 + (7000 - Lure.y));
draw_rectangle_colour(0, 4500, room_width, dy, background_color, background_color, c_black, c_black, 0);

if (LightingSystem.day_coefficient > 0)
    draw_rectangle_colour(0, -2000, room_width, 1294, sky_color, sky_color, sky_color, sky_color, 0);

if (LightingSystem.day_coefficient < 1)
{
    draw_set_alpha(1 - LightingSystem.day_coefficient);
    draw_rectangle_colour(0, 0, room_width, 1294, sky_color_dark, sky_color_dark, sky_color_dark, sky_color_dark, 0);
    draw_set_alpha(1);
}

if (LightingSystem.lightning_flash > 0)
{
    draw_set_alpha(LightingSystem.lightning_flash);
    draw_set_color(LightingSystem.lightning_color);
    draw_rectangle(0, 0, room_width, 1294, false);
    draw_set_alpha(1);
    draw_set_color(c_white);
}

alpha = 1 - power(1 - clamp(LightingSystem.day_coefficient * 2, 0, 1), 3);
alpha_dark = 1 - power(clamp((LightingSystem.day_coefficient * 2) - 1, 0, 1), 3);
building_scalar = clamp(((Camera.x - Arcade.x) + 150) / 200, -1, 1);

for (i = 0; i < nlayers; i++)
{
    if (Arcade.visible && i == (nlayers - 1))
        draw_sprite_ext(spr_building_arcade_back, 0, Arcade.x + (building_scalar * 100), Arcade.y + 60, 0.5, 0.5, 0, c_white, 1);
    
    offset_x = (view_xview[view_current] / layer_depth[i]) % (bg_width * bg_scale);
    n_repeats = floor(view_xview[view_current] / layer_depth[i] / (bg_width * bg_scale));
    offset_y = (view_yview[view_current] - vertical_view_base) * -0.25 * (nlayers - i - 0.5);
    offset_y = max(offset_y, -400);
    dx = view_xview[view_current] - offset_x;
    dy = (1324 + layer_height[i]) - offset_y;
    
    if (i == 0 && x > 22000)
        dy += ((x - 22000) / 100);
    
    if (i == (nlayers - 4))
    {
        with (LaunchPlatform)
        {
            lp_offset = (Camera.x - LaunchPlatform.x) / (other.layer_depth[other.i] + 0.1);
            draw_sprite_ext(sprite_index, image_index, Camera.x - lp_offset, y - other.offset_y, 0.5, 0.5, image_angle, c_white, image_alpha);
        }
    }
    
    if (LightingSystem.day_coefficient > 0)
    {
        if (n_repeats < layer_repeats[i])
        {
            draw_sprite_ext(bg_sprite, i, dx, dy, bg_scale, bg_scale, 0, c_white, alpha);
            
            if (n_repeats < (layer_repeats[i] - 1))
                draw_sprite_ext(bg_sprite, i, dx + (bg_width * bg_scale), dy, bg_scale, bg_scale, 0, c_white, alpha);
            else
                draw_sprite_ext(bg_sprite_end, i, dx + (bg_width * bg_scale), dy, bg_scale, bg_scale, 0, c_white, alpha);
        }
        else if (n_repeats == layer_repeats[i])
        {
            draw_sprite_ext(bg_sprite_end, i, dx, dy, bg_scale, bg_scale, 0, c_white, alpha);
        }
    }
    
    if (LightingSystem.day_coefficient < 1)
    {
        if (n_repeats < layer_repeats[i])
        {
            draw_sprite_ext(bg_sprite_dark, i, dx, dy, bg_scale, bg_scale, 0, c_white, alpha_dark);
            
            if (n_repeats < (layer_repeats[i] - 1))
                draw_sprite_ext(bg_sprite_dark, i, dx + (bg_width * bg_scale), dy, bg_scale, bg_scale, 0, c_white, alpha_dark);
            else
                draw_sprite_ext(bg_sprite_dark_end, i, dx + (bg_width * bg_scale), dy, bg_scale, bg_scale, 0, c_white, alpha_dark);
        }
        else if (n_repeats == layer_repeats[i])
        {
            draw_sprite_ext(bg_sprite_dark_end, i, dx, dy, bg_scale, bg_scale, 0, c_white, alpha_dark);
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Problematicus_Other_13.gml =====
action_inherited();
fatigue = 0;
target_angle = point_direction(x, y, trail.x, trail.y);

if (stamina > 100)
{
    isDragging = 1;
}
else
{
    isDragging = 0;
    stamina_recharge++;
    
    if (recharge_length < stamina_recharge)
    {
        if (stamina_recharge_uses > 0)
        {
            stamina_recharge = 0;
            stamina_recharge_uses--;
            stamina = MAX_STAMINA - (20 * stamina_recharge_uses);
        }
    }
}

if (!isDragging)
{
    thrall_ang_force = 0.017;
    thrall_friction = 0.9;
}
else if (abs(angle_difference(image_angle + 180, target_angle)) > 45)
{
    thrall_force += ((10 - thrall_force) * 0.68);
    thrall_ang_force = 0.03;
    thrall_friction = 0.8;
}
else
{
    if (thrall_force > 60)
        thrall_force = 60;
    else
        thrall_force++;
    
    thrall_ang_force = 0.004;
    thrall_friction = 0.2;
}

if (trail > 0)
{
    if (point_distance(x, y, trail.x, trail.y) > 200)
        trail.path_speed = 5;
    else
        trail.path_speed = 50;
    
    if (trail.reachedEnd)
        trail.path_speed = 0;
    
    if (Lure.fowled)
        trail.path_speed = 0;
    
    if (!isDragging)
        trail.path_speed = 0;
}

full_reset = 0;

{
    nmags = 5 + min(0, 2 - ang_index);
    
    for (mag_index = 0; mag_index < nmags; mag_index++)
    {
        skipped_cells = max(0, -2 + ang_index);
        angheight = 50 / (3 + mag_index + skipped_cells);
        tdir = (angheight * ang_index) + random(angheight * 0.3) + (angheight * 0.7 * ((mag_index + skipped_cells) % 2));
        column_start = grid_start + (column_width * skipped_cells);
        tdist = column_start + (column_width * mag_index) + COLUMN_BUFFER;
        
        if (mag_index > 0)
            tdist += random(column_width - COLUMN_BUFFER);
        
        tx = lengthdir_x(tdist, -tdir);
        ty = lengthdir_y(tdist, -tdir);
        nodes[ang_index, mag_index] = instance_create(x + tx, y + ty, Node);
        nodes[ang_index, mag_index].tier = 1 + (ang_index * 2);
        nodes[ang_index, mag_index].column = mag_index + 1;
        
        if (mag_index > 0)
            nodes[ang_index, mag_index].c1 = nodes[ang_index, mag_index - 1];
        
        if (mag_index > 1)
        {
            nodes[ang_index, mag_index].c2 = nodes[ang_index, mag_index - 2];
            nodes[ang_index, mag_index].tier += 1;
        }
    }
}
