===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Red_Skitter_Other_10.gml =====
fish_lunge();

with (dataobject)
{
    if (other.isVisible)
    {
        nearest[0] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishMedium);
        nearest[1] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishLarge);
        
        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        {
            square_distance = power(nearest[i].x - other.x, 2) + power(nearest[i].y - other.y, 2);
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min))
                square_distance = fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min;
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_cutoff * fish_distance_cutoff))
            {
                mag = forceScalar[i] / square_distance;
                dir = point_direction(nearest[i].x, nearest[i].y, other.x, other.y);
                other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
                other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
            }
        }
    }
    
    square_distance = power(other.start_x - other.x, 2) + power(other.start_y - other.y, 2);
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_max * home_distance_max))
        square_distance = home_distance_max * home_distance_max;
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_cutoff * home_distance_cutoff))
    {
        mag = forceScalar[i] * square_distance;
        dir = point_direction(other.x, other.y, other.start_x, other.start_y);
        other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
        other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
    }
}

hspeed *= 0.99;
vspeed *= 0.99;

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Rocket_Step_2.gml =====
action_inherited();

if (Lure.state == 0)
    fuel = MAX_FUEL;

if (Lure.state == 1)
{
    if (!target_distance_is_set)
    {
        target_distance_is_set = 1;
        target_distance[Rod.rod_id] += Girl.x;
    }
    
    if (Lure.hspeed > 0)
        time = (target_distance[Rod.rod_id] - Lure.x) / Lure.hspeed;
    else
        time = 1800;
    
    delta_y = 1244 - Lure.y;
    target_y_velocity = (delta_y / time) + (-0.5 * Lure.LURE_GRAVITY * time);
    image_angle = 0;
    
    if (fuel > 0 && Lure.x < target_distance[Rod.rod_id] && Lure.vspeed > target_y_velocity && delta_y > 0 && Lure.hspeed > 0)
    {
        if (!audio_is_playing(sfx_rocket_burn))
            audio_play_sound(sfx_rocket_burn, 0, true);
        
        Lure.rocket_thrust = THRUST * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar);
        burn_burst_at_xy_with_vel(x - lengthdir_x(Lure.speed, Lure.direction), (y - lengthdir_y(Lure.speed, Lure.direction)) + 30, Lure.direction, Lure.speed);
        fuel -= (1 * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
        burning = 1;
    }
    else
    {
        audio_stop_sound(sfx_rocket_burn);
        burning = 0;
    }
}
else if (Lure.state == 2)
{
    audio_stop_sound(sfx_rocket_burn);
    burn_clear();
    instance_destroy();
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_RodCard_Other_10.gml =====
action_inherited();
closeMenu = 0;

if (Rod.rod_id == item_id)
{
    with (RodMenu)
        instance_destroy();
    
    instance_create(0, 0, UpgradeSlotMenu);
}
else
{
    if (!RodData.unlocked[item_id])
    {
        closeMenu = 1;
        
        if (Girl.dollars >= RodData.cost[item_id])
        {
            RodData.unlocked[item_id] = 1;
            
            for (i = 1; i <= 2; i++)
            {
                if ((item_id + i) < RodData.nItems)
                {
                    if (RodData.unlocked[item_id + i] == -1)
                        RodData.unlocked[item_id + i] = 0;
                }
            }
            
            Girl.dollars -= RodData.cost[item_id];
            rod_was_unlocked(item_id);
        }
        else
        {
            instance_create(0, 0, Message_MoreMoney);
        }
    }
    
    if (RodData.unlocked[item_id])
    {
        Rod.rod_id = item_id;
        
        with (Rod)
            event_perform(ev_other, ev_user0);
    }
    
    if (closeMenu)
    {
        with (RodMenu)
            instance_destroy();
    }
}

                break;

                repels++;
                break;

                upgrades[3] = 1;

                upgrades[4] = 1;
                break;

                break;

                upgrades[11]++;
                sparks++;
                break;

                upgrades[12]++;
                weeds++;
                break;

                upgrades[14] = 1;

            case 12:
                upgrades[15] = 1;

        {

            base_lunge_offset += 100;
        }

    for (upg = 8; upg <= 10; upg++)
    {

        {
            base_lunge_mod *= power(0.5, Girl.gear_repel_scalar);
            base_lunge_offset -= 60;
        }

    base_lunge_mod *= Girl.gear_lunge_scalar[display_size - 1];
    
    if (Girl.gear_target_fish == object_id)
        base_lunge_mod *= Girl.gear_target_lunge_scalar;

    base_lunge_mod *= power(0.8, Rod.upgrades[12]);

}

if (gamepad_is_supported() && gamepad_is_connected(0))
{
    if (gamepad_button_check_pressed(0, gp_shoulderrb))
        event_perform(ev_other, ev_user5);
    
    if (gamepad_button_check_released(0, gp_shoulderrb))
        event_perform(ev_other, ev_user6);
}

if (!rodDataLoaded)
{
    event_perform(ev_other, ev_user0);
    rodDataLoaded = 1;
}

switch (state)
{
    case 0:
        break;
    
    case 1:
        if (angle < BACKSWING_MAXANGLE)
        {
            angle += (BACKSWING_SPEED * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
        }
        else
        {
            angle = BACKSWING_MAXANGLE;
            state = 2;
            playedSwish = 0;
        }
        
        break;
    
    case 2:
        if (angle > 0)
        {
            angle -= (CAST_SPEED * (Girl.DELTA_INSTANT * Girl.time_scalar));
        }
        else
        {
            angle = 0;
            
            if (Lure.state == 0)
                state = 0;
            else
                state = 3;
        }
        
        if (angle < 100 && !playedSwish)
        {
            playedSwish = 1;
            audio_play_sound(sfx_rod_swish, 0, false);
        }
        
        break;
    
    case 3:
        break;
}

image_angle = angle;

    current_datetime = date_current_datetime();
    milliseconds_elapsed = date_second_span(saved_datetime, current_datetime) * 1000;
    days_elapsed = date_day_span(saved_datetime, current_datetime);
    
    if (date_get_day_of_year(saved_datetime) == date_get_day_of_year(current_datetime))
        same_day = 1;
    else
        same_day = 0;

        if (torby_are_extinct)
            steam_set_achievement("extinction");
        
        RealismMode = ini_read_real("Main", "realism", 0);
        n_screenshots = ini_read_real("Main", "nscreens", 0);
        dollars = ini_read_real("Main", "dollars", 0);
        Ancient_Kingfish.hasBeenSold = ini_read_real("Main", "kingsold", 0);
        level = ini_read_real("Main", "level", 0);
        experience = ini_read_real("Main", "experience", 0);
        legacy += ini_read_real("Main", "leg", 0);
        var ncounters = array_length_1d(counter);
        
        for (i = 0; i < ncounters; i++)
            counter[i] = ini_read_real("Main", "counter" + string(i), 0);
        
        wasRealismPlayed = ini_read_real("Main", "RealismPlayed", 0);
        
        if (!wasRealismPlayed && Girl.counter[8] > 31)
            Girl.counter[8] = 31;
    }

    {
        gear = instance_create(x, y, GearData.gear[gear_id]);
        gear.gear_id = gear_id;
        
        with (gear)
            gear_equip();
    }
    
    with (Fish)
    {
        state = ini_read_real("Fishes", string(id), 0);
        
        if (state < 0)
        {
            respawn_time_saved = state * -1;
            rt = respawn_time_saved - SaveManager.milliseconds_elapsed;
            
            if (rt > 0)
            {
                old_depth = depth;
                life = 0;
                fish_die();
                respawn_time = rt;
            }
            else
            {
                state = 0;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            state = 0;
        }
    }
    
    ini_section_delete("Fishes");
    
    with (Fish)
    {
        switch (state)
        {
            case 6:
                ini_write_real("Fishes", string(id), -1 * (respawn_time - current_time));
                break;
            
            default:
                ini_write_real("Fishes", string(id), state);
                break;
        }
    }

            unlocked = u;

        Rod.rod_id = active_rod;

        for (i = 0; i < nItems; i++)
            unlocked[i] = ini_read_real("Unlocks", "lure" + string(i), -1);
    }

    {
        for (i = 0; i < nItems; i++)
        {
            unlocked[i] = ini_read_real("Unlocks", "boat" + string(i), -1);

            {
                Girl.bestBoat = i;
                steam_set_achievement("boat_" + string(i));
            }
        }
    }
    
    var bi = ini_read_real("Main", "boat", -1);
    
    if (bi > 0 && BoatData.object[bi] > 0)
        instance_create(0, 0, BoatData.object[bi]);
    
    if (instance_exists(Boat))
    {
        n_fish_in_boat = ini_read_real("OnBoat", "n_fish", 0);
        
        for (i = 0; i < n_fish_in_boat; i++)
        {
            ind = ini_read_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(i) + "id", -1);
            var obj = ini_read_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(i) + "ind", -1);
            
            if (obj > 0)
            {
                var f;
                
                if (instance_exists(ind))
                {
                    if (ind.object_index == obj)
                        f = ind;
                }
                else
                {
                    var found = 0;
                    var try = 1;
                    var cf_i = 0;
                    
                    while (!found && try)
                    {
                        f = instance_find(obj, cf_i);
                        cf_i++;
                        
                        if (instance_exists(f))
                        {
                            if (f.state != 4)
                                found = 1;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            show_message("Some fish on boat may be hollow.");
                            try = 0;
                            f = -1;
                        }
                    }
                }
                
                if (f < 0 || !instance_exists(f))
                    f = instance_create(10000, 10000, obj);
                
                if (object_is_ancestor(f.object_index, Fish))
                {
                    with (f)
                    {
                        fish_appraise();
                        Boat.fish_count++;
                        Boat.fish_value += value;
                        Girl.nFishToSell++;
                        fish_kill_path();
                    }
                    
                    f.stuck = 1;
                    f.onBoat = 1;
                    f.stick_target = Boat.id;
                    f.stick_dist = ini_read_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(i) + "sdist", 0);
                    f.life = ini_read_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(i) + "sl", 0);
                    f.stick_angle = ini_read_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(i) + "sang", 0);
                    f.stick_x_offset = ini_read_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(i) + "sxo", 0);
                    f.stick_y_offset = ini_read_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(i) + "sxy", 0);
                    f.depth = ini_read_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(i) + "depth", 0);
                    f.netherFish = 1;
                    f.state = 4;
                }
                else
                {
                    show_message("That's not a fish...");
                    
                    with (f)
                        instance_destroy();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    n_lamps = ini_read_real("Lamp", "n_lamps", 0);
    
    for (i = 0; i < n_lamps; i++)
    {
        ind = Bulbfish.object_index;
        var f = instance_create(-1000, -1000, ind);
        
        with (f)
        {
            x = ini_read_real("Lamp", "f" + string(other.i) + "x", x);
            y = ini_read_real("Lamp", "f" + string(other.i) + "y", y);
            depth = ini_read_real("Lamp", "f" + string(other.i) + "depth", 0);
            netherFish = 1;
            state = 7;
            lamp_x = x;
            lamp_y = y;
            slide_x = 0;
            slide_y = 0;
            
            if (light2 < 0)
                light2 = instance_create(x, y, Light);
            
            if (x < 0)
                instance_destroy(id);
        }
    }
    
    lf = ini_read_real("LF", "f", -1);
    var ind = ini_read_real("LF", "fid", -1);
    Lure.size = ini_read_real("LF", "size", Lure.size);
    
    if (lf > 0)
    {
        if (instance_exists(ind))
        {
            if (ind.object_index == lf)
                lure_fish = ind;
        }
        else
        {
            var found = 0;
            var try = 1;
            var cf_i = 0;
            
            while (!found && try)
            {
                lure_fish = instance_find(lf, cf_i);
                cf_i++;
                
                if (instance_exists(lure_fish))
                {
                    if (lure_fish.state != 4)
                    {
                        found = 1;
                        lure_fish.image_yscale = image_xscale;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    show_message("Fish on lure is hollow.");
                    try = 0;
                    lure_fish = -1;
                }
            }
        }
        
        if (lure_fish < 0 || !instance_exists(lure_fish))
            lure_fish = instance_create(10000, 10000, lf);
        
        if (object_is_ancestor(lure_fish.object_index, Fish))
        {
            with (lure_fish)
            {
                hasGivenExperience = 1;
                image_yscale = 0.5;
                name = fish_object_to_name(object_index);
                fish_appraise();
                state = 3;
                SaveManager.loadingFish = 1;
                event_perform(ev_other, ev_user7);
                SaveManager.loadingFish = 0;
                state = 3;
            }
        }
    }
    
    ini_write_real("Lamp", "n_lamps", n_lamps);
    
    with (ToolData)
    {
        for (i = 0; i < nItems; i++)
        {
            unlocked[i] = ini_read_real("Unlocks", "tool" + string(i), -1);
            
            if (unlocked[i])
            {
                ToolData.item[i].active = 1;
                steam_set_achievement("tool_" + string(i + 1));
            }
        }
    }
    
    with (Analytics)
    {
        for (i = 0; i < nGroups; i++)
            ABGroup[i] = 0;
        
        ABGroupName = ini_read_string("ABGroups", "name", "DEFAULT_ERROR");
    }
    
    if (same_day)
        QuestEngine.quests_accepted_today = ini_read_real("Quest2", "accepted", 0);
    
    for (i = 0; i <= Girl.TRUE_MAX_QUESTS; i++)
    {
        questType = ini_read_real("Quest2", "type" + string(i), -1);
        questID = ini_read_real("Quest2", "id" + string(i), -1);
        questProgress = ini_read_real("Quest2", "progress" + string(i), 0);
        
        if (questID > 0)
        {
            q = start_quest(questType, questID);
            q.progress = questProgress;
        }
    }
    
    with (CatalogData)
    {
        i = array_height_2d(name) - 1;
        
        while (i >= 0)
        {
            j = array_length_2d(name, i) - 1;
            
            while (j >= 0)
            {
                count[i, j] = ini_read_real("Catalog", "f" + string(i) + string(j), -1);
                
                if (count[i, j] > 0)
                    default_life = 3;
                else
                    default_life = -1;
                
                life[i, j] = ini_read_real("Catalog", "li" + string(i) + string(j), default_life);
                pop[i, j] = ini_read_real("Catalog", "pop" + string(i) + string(j), -1);
                
                if (SaveManager.days_elapsed >= 3)
                {
                    if (pop[i, j] <= 0)
                        pop[i, j] = 0;
                    
                    if (pop[i, j] > 0)
                        pop[i, j] = 1000;
                }
                
                if (pop[i, j] >= 0)
                    pop_loaded = 1;
                else if (pop[i, j] > 0)
                    pop[i, j] = 1000;
                
                j--;
            }
            
            i--;
        }
    }
    
    with (UpgradeData)
    {
        for (i = 0; i <= 17; i++)
            unlocks[i] = ini_read_real("Upgrades", "unlocks" + string(i), unlocks[i]);
        
        for (ix = 0; ix < 6; ix++)
        {
            for (iy = 0; iy < 8; iy++)
            {
                upg[ix, iy] = ini_read_real("Upgrades", "upg" + string(ix) + string(iy), upg[ix, iy]);
                object[ix, iy] = ini_read_real("Upgrades", "obj" + string(ix) + string(iy), -1);
            }
        }
    }
    
    with (GearData)
    {
        for (i = 0; i < n_gear; i++)
            unlocked[i] = ini_read_real("Hatalog", "hat" + string(i), 0);
    }
    
    if (ini_read_real("Breakables", "A", 0))
    {
        with (BreakableRockA)
            instance_destroy();
    }
    
    if (ini_read_real("Breakables", "B", 0))
    {
        with (BreakableRockB)
            instance_destroy();
    }
    
    if (ini_read_real("Breakables", "C", 0))
    {
        with (BreakableRockC)
            instance_destroy();
    }
}
else
{
    with (Analytics)
        event_perform(ev_other, ev_user0);
    
    instance_create(0, 0, GameModeMenu);
}

ini_close();

with (Analytics)
    event_perform(ev_other, ev_user1);

saving_locked = 0;

            ini_write_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(other.n_fish_in_boat) + "id", id + 158934279);
            ini_write_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(other.n_fish_in_boat) + "sdist", stick_dist);
            ini_write_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(other.n_fish_in_boat) + "sang", stick_angle);
            ini_write_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(other.n_fish_in_boat) + "sxo", stick_x_offset);
            ini_write_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(other.n_fish_in_boat) + "sxy", stick_y_offset);
            ini_write_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(other.n_fish_in_boat) + "depth", depth);
            ini_write_real("OnBoat", "f" + string(other.n_fish_in_boat) + "sl", life);
            other.n_fish_in_boat++;
        }
    }
    
    ini_write_real("OnBoat", "n_fish", n_fish_in_boat);

            ini_write_real("Lamp", "f" + string(other.n_fish_in_boat) + "y", y);
            ini_write_real("Lamp", "f" + string(other.n_fish_in_boat) + "depth", depth);
            other.n_lamps++;
        }
    }
    
    ini_write_real("Lamp", "n_lamps", n_lamps);

    {

        ini_write_real("LF", "fid", Lure.fish.id);
        ini_write_real("LF", "size", Lure.size);
    }

        ini_write_real("LF", "size", Lure.size);

        {

            ini_write_real("Unlocks", "rod" + string(i), unlocked[i]);

    }

    else
        ini_write_real("Main", "boat", -1);
    
    with (LureData)
    {
        for (i = 0; i < nItems; i++)
            ini_write_real("Unlocks", "lure" + string(i), unlocked[i]);
    }
    
    with (ToolData)
    {
        for (i = 0; i < nItems; i++)
            ini_write_real("Unlocks", "tool" + string(i), unlocked[i]);
    }
    
    ini_write_real("Quest2", "accepted", QuestEngine.quests_accepted_today);
    ini_write_real("Quest2", "today", date_get_day_of_year(date_current_datetime()));
    qc = 0;
    
    with (Quest)
    {
        other.qc++;
        
        if (!complete)
        {
            ini_write_real("Quest2", "type" + string(other.qc), type);
            ini_write_real("Quest2", "id" + string(other.qc), index);
            ini_write_real("Quest2", "progress" + string(other.qc), progress);
        }
        else
        {
            ini_write_real("Quest2", "type" + string(other.qc), -1);
            ini_write_real("Quest2", "id" + string(other.qc), -1);
            ini_write_real("Quest2", "progress" + string(other.qc), 0);
        }
    }
    
    i = qc + 1;
    
    while (i <= Girl.TRUE_MAX_QUESTS)
    {
        ini_write_real("Quest2", "type" + string(i), -1);
        ini_write_real("Quest2", "id" + string(i), -1);
        ini_write_real("Quest2", "progress" + string(i), 0);
        i++;
    }
    
    with (CatalogData)
    {
        i = array_height_2d(name) - 1;
        
        while (i >= 0)
        {
            j = array_length_2d(name, i) - 1;
            
            while (j >= 0)
            {
                ini_write_real("Catalog", "f" + string(i) + string(j), count[i, j]);
                ini_write_real("Catalog", "li" + string(i) + string(j), life[i, j]);
                ini_write_real("Catalog", "pop" + string(i) + string(j), pop[i, j]);
                j--;
            }
            
            i--;
        }
    }
    
    with (UpgradeData)
    {
        for (i = 0; i <= 17; i++)
            ini_write_real("Upgrades", "unlocks" + string(i), unlocks[i]);
        
        for (ix = 0; ix < 6; ix++)
        {
            for (iy = 0; iy < 8; iy++)
            {
                ini_write_real("Upgrades", "upg" + string(ix) + string(iy), upg[ix, iy]);
                ini_write_real("Upgrades", "obj" + string(ix) + string(iy), object[ix, iy]);
            }
        }
    }
    
    with (GearData)
    {
        for (i = 0; i < n_gear; i++)
            ini_write_real("Hatalog", "hat" + string(i), unlocked[i]);
    }
    
    rockA_destroyed = !instance_exists(BreakableRockA);
    ini_write_real("Breakables", "A", rockA_destroyed);
    rockB_destroyed = !instance_exists(BreakableRockB);
    ini_write_real("Breakables", "B", rockB_destroyed);
    rockC_destroyed = !instance_exists(BreakableRockC);
    ini_write_real("Breakables", "C", rockC_destroyed);
    ini_close();
    ini_open("meta.ini");
    ini_write_real("save" + string(saveflag), "nss", 1);
    ini_write_real("save" + string(saveflag), "Used", 1);
    ini_write_real("save" + string(saveflag), "Level", Girl.level);
    ini_write_real("save" + string(saveflag), "Dollars", Girl.dollars);
    
    if (Girl.RealismMode)
        ini_write_string("save" + string(saveflag), "Mode", "Realism");
    else
        ini_write_string("save" + string(saveflag), "Mode", "Classic");
    
    if (Girl.gear > 0)
        ini_write_string("save" + string(saveflag), "Hat", Girl.gear.name);
    else
        ini_write_string("save" + string(saveflag), "Hat", "No hat.");
    
    ini_close();
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_SceneItem_Step_2.gml =====
scene_depth = depth + 18;

if (depth < -18 && (object_index == Weed || object_index == Dangler))
    scene_depth += 1;

if (scene_depth > 18)
    scene_depth -= 18;

if (scene_depth > 0)
    scene_depth *= 0.1;
else
    scene_depth *= 0.5;

x = home_x + ((Camera.x - home_x) * scene_depth * 1 * para_scale);
y = home_y + ((Camera.y - home_y) * scene_depth * 0.1 * para_scale);

        wake_fish = 1;

            wake_time = -1;
            CatSwim.image_index += 1;
            awake = 1;
        }
    }
}

with (dataobject)
{
    if (other.isVisible)
    {
        nearest[0] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishNotSmall);
        nearest[1] = nearest[0];
        distance_to_nearest = 200;
        cc = other.id;
        
        with (Skitter)
        {
            if (id != other.cc && other.distance_to_nearest > point_distance(x, y, other.cc.x, other.cc.y))
            {
                other.distance_to_nearest = point_distance(x, y, other.cc.x, other.cc.y);
                other.nearest[1] = id;
            }
        }
        
        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        {
            square_distance = power(nearest[i].x - other.x, 2) + power(nearest[i].y - other.y, 2);
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min))
                square_distance = fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min;
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_cutoff * fish_distance_cutoff))
            {
                mag = forceScalar[i] / square_distance;
                dir = point_direction(nearest[i].x, nearest[i].y, other.x, other.y);
                other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
                other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
            }
        }
    }
    
    square_distance = power(other.start_x - other.x, 2) + power(other.start_y - other.y, 2);
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_max * home_distance_max))
        square_distance = home_distance_max * home_distance_max;
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_cutoff * home_distance_cutoff))
    {
        mag = forceScalar[2] * square_distance;
        dir = point_direction(other.x, other.y, other.start_x, other.start_y);
        other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
        other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
    }
}

    else if (roll < 0.26)
    {
        fish_target_random_living_fish(32);
    }
    else if (roll < 0.3)
    {
        fish_target_random_living_fish(247);
    }
    else
    {
        target = -1;
        start_x = x + (sign(hspeed) * 5000);
        start_y = room_height + 1000;
    }
}

if (stunned <= 0)
{
    if (target < 0)
    {
        if (y < 5000)
            vspeed += 0.2;
        
        speed = max(3, speed);
        fish_flock(130, 0.03);
    }
    else if (y > 5000)
    {
        vspeed -= 0.2;
    }
    else if (point_distance(x, y, target_x, target_y) > 30)
    {
        fish_elast_towards_target(0.1, 0.85);
    }
    else
    {
        target_reached = 1;
        fish_elast_towards_target(0.1, 0.85);
        speed = 0.11;
    }
}
else
{
    stunned--;
}

fish_avoid_wall(130);

if (collided)
    stunned = 90;

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Sodafish_Draw_0.gml =====
if (view_current != 3)
{
    harness_angle = sin(current_time / 8000);
    
    if (image_yscale < 0)
    {
        ang_offset = 0;
        h_off_scalar = -1;
        harness_angle += 180;
    }
    else
    {
        ang_offset = 0;
        h_off_scalar = 1;
    }
    
    harness_x = x + lengthdir_x(harness_off_mag, image_angle + (harness_off_ang * h_off_scalar) + ang_offset);
    harness_y = y + lengthdir_y(harness_off_mag, image_angle + (harness_off_ang * h_off_scalar) + ang_offset);
    can_offset_angle = image_angle + harness_angle + can_offset_angle_bias;
    can_x = harness_x + lengthdir_x(can_offset_distance - can_slide, can_offset_angle);
    can_y = harness_y + lengthdir_y(can_offset_distance - can_slide, can_offset_angle);
    
    if (point_distance(x, y, tube_x, tube_y) > tube_jitter)
    {
        tube_direction = point_direction(x, y, tube_x, tube_y);
        tube_x = x + lengthdir_x(tube_jitter, tube_direction);
        tube_y = y + lengthdir_y(tube_jitter, tube_direction);
    }
    
    tube_x_vel += (tube_elast * (x - tube_x));
    tube_y_vel += (tube_elast * (y - tube_y));
    tube_x_vel *= tube_drag;
    tube_y_vel *= tube_drag;
    tube_x += tube_x_vel;
    tube_y += tube_y_vel;
    
    if (state == 1)
        cheeky = 1;
    else
        cheeky = 0;
    
    if (random(1) < (1/120))
        face_index = irandom(1);
    
    if ((abs(angle_difference(direction, 0)) < 45 && image_yscale > 0) || (abs(angle_difference(direction, 180)) < 45 && image_yscale < 0))
    {
        can_slide += 0.1;
        
        if (can_slide > 20)
        {
            can_slide = 20;
            
            if (sodaCan < 0)
            {
                sodaCan = instance_create(can_x, can_y, Soda);
                sodaCan.image_angle = image_angle + harness_angle;
                sodaCan.image_xscale = image_xscale;
                sodaCan.image_yscale = abs(image_yscale);
                face_index = 9;
                
                if (image_yscale < 0)
                    sodaCan.depth = depth + 0.1;
                else
                    sodaCan.depth = depth - 0.1;
            }
        }
    }
    else
    {
        can_slide--;
        
        if (can_slide < 0)
            can_slide = 0;
    }
    
    fish_predraw_switchcase();
    
    if (has_tubes)
    {
        tube_index = 0;
        
        if (image_yscale < 0)
            tube_index = 1;
        
        draw_sprite_ext(tube_sprite, tube_index, tube_x, tube_y, image_xscale, image_yscale, image_angle, c_white, 1);
    }
    
    if (has_tubes && image_yscale < 0)
    {
        if (sodaCan < 0)
            draw_sprite_ext(spr_fish_m27_soda, 0, can_x, can_y, image_xscale, abs(image_yscale), image_angle + harness_angle, c_white, 1);
    }
    
    if (life < 3)
    {
        image_index = bone_index - life;
        draw_sprite_ext(body_sprite, bone_index, x, y, image_xscale, image_yscale, image_angle, c_white, 1);
    }
    
    if (life > 0)
    {
        draw_sprite_ext(body_sprite, image_index, x, y, image_xscale, image_yscale, image_angle, image_blend, 1);
        draw_sprite_ext(face_sprite, face_index, x, y, image_xscale, image_yscale, image_angle, image_blend, 1);
    }
    
    if (has_tubes)
    {
        draw_sprite_ext(tube_sprite, 1 - tube_index, tube_x, tube_y, image_xscale, image_yscale, image_angle, c_white, 1);
        
        if (!tube_index)
            draw_sprite_ext(tube_sprite, 2, x, y, image_xscale, image_yscale, image_angle, c_white, 1);
    }
    
    if (has_tubes)
    {
        if (image_yscale > 0)
        {
            draw_sprite_ext(harness_sprite, 0, harness_x, harness_y, image_xscale, abs(image_yscale), image_angle + harness_angle, c_white, 1);
            
            if (sodaCan < 0)
                draw_sprite_ext(spr_fish_m27_soda, 0, can_x, can_y, image_xscale, abs(image_yscale), image_angle + harness_angle, c_white, 1);
            
            draw_sprite_ext(harness_sprite, 1, harness_x, harness_y, image_xscale, abs(image_yscale), image_angle + harness_angle, c_white, 1);
        }
        else
        {
            draw_sprite_ext(harness_sprite, 2, harness_x, harness_y, image_xscale, abs(image_yscale), image_angle + harness_angle, c_white, 1);
        }
    }
}

with (dataobject)
{
    if (other.isVisible)
    {
        nearest[0] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishMedium);
        nearest[1] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishLarge);
        
        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        {
            square_distance = power(nearest[i].x - other.x, 2) + power(nearest[i].y - other.y, 2);
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min))
                square_distance = fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min;
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_cutoff * fish_distance_cutoff))
            {
                mag = forceScalar[i] / square_distance;
                dir = point_direction(nearest[i].x, nearest[i].y, other.x, other.y);
                other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
                other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
            }
        }
    }
    
    square_distance = power(other.start_x - other.x, 2) + power(other.start_y - other.y, 2);
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_max * home_distance_max))
        square_distance = home_distance_max * home_distance_max;
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_cutoff * home_distance_cutoff))
    {
        mag = forceScalar[2] * square_distance;
        dir = point_direction(other.x, other.y, other.start_x, other.start_y);
        other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
        other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
    }
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Object_Trillow_Other_10.gml =====
fish_lunge();

with (dataobject)
{
    if (other.isVisible)
    {
        nearest[0] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishMedium);
        nearest[1] = instance_nearest(other.x, other.y, FishLarge);
        
        for (i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        {
            square_distance = power(nearest[i].x - other.x, 2) + power(nearest[i].y - other.y, 2);
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min))
                square_distance = fish_distance_min * fish_distance_min;
            
            if (square_distance < (fish_distance_cutoff * fish_distance_cutoff))
            {
                mag = forceScalar[i] / square_distance;
                dir = point_direction(nearest[i].x, nearest[i].y, other.x, other.y);
                other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
                other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
            }
        }
    }
    
    square_distance = power(other.start_x - other.x, 2) + power(other.start_y - other.y, 2);
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_max * home_distance_max))
        square_distance = home_distance_max * home_distance_max;
    
    if (square_distance > (home_distance_cutoff * home_distance_cutoff))
    {
        mag = forceScalar[2] * square_distance;
        dir = point_direction(other.x, other.y, other.start_x, other.start_y);
        other.hspeed += lengthdir_x(mag, dir);
        other.vspeed += lengthdir_y(mag, dir);
    }
}

hspeed *= 0.99;
vspeed *= 0.99;

if (speed > 10)
    speed = 10;

if (speed < 1)
    speed += 0.1;

if (y < 1294)
    vspeed += 1;

if (x < 1000)
    hspeed += 1;

loop_angle = 360 * (((current_time % loop_time) / loop_time) + loop_offset);
start_x = TRUE_START_X + lengthdir_x(loop_radius, loop_angle);
start_y = TRUE_START_Y + lengthdir_y(loop_radius, loop_angle);

action_inherited();
cost = calculate_upgrade_cost(card_id, Rod.rod_id);
allowPurchase = 1;

switch (card_id)
{
    case 0:
        if (Rod.scouts >= 1)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 1:
        if (Rod.weights >= 8)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 2:
        if (Rod.feathers >= 2)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 4:
        if (Rod.attracts >= 3)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 5:
        if (Rod.repels >= 3)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 6:
        if (Rod.upgrades[3] == 1)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 7:
        if (Rod.upgrades[4] == 1)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 8:
        if (Rod.upgrades[13] >= 8)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 9:
        if (Rod.upgrades[11] >= 3)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 10:
        if (Rod.upgrades[12] >= 3)
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 11:
        if (Rod.upgrades[14])
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 12:
        if (Rod.upgrades[15])
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 13:
        if (Rod.upgrades[16])
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 14:
        if (Rod.upgrades[18])
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 15:
        if (Rod.upgrades[19])
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 16:
        if (Rod.upgrades[20])
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
    
    case 17:
        if (Rod.upgrades[21])
            allowPurchase = 0;
        
        break;
}

if (!allowPurchase)
{
    instance_create(0, 0, Message_TooManyUpgrades);
}
else if (Girl.dollars >= cost)
{
    if (card_id == 9 && Rod.upgrades[12] > 0)
    {
        instance_create(0, 0, Message_NoDullGlitter);
        allowPurchase = 0;
    }
    
    if (card_id == 10 && Rod.upgrades[11] > 0)
    {
        instance_create(0, 0, Message_NoDullGlitter);
        allowPurchase = 0;
    }
    
    if (card_id == 4 || card_id == 5)
    {
        if (Lure.fish > 0)
        {
            if (card_id == 4)
            {
                if (Rod.upgrades[8] == Lure.fish.object_id || Rod.upgrades[9] == Lure.fish.object_id || Rod.upgrades[10] == Lure.fish.object_id)
                    allowPurchase = 0;
            }
            else if (Rod.upgrades[5] == Lure.fish.object_id || Rod.upgrades[6] == Lure.fish.object_id || Rod.upgrades[7] == Lure.fish.object_id)
            {
                allowPurchase = 0;
            }
            
            if (!allowPurchase)
                instance_create(0, 0, Message_AttractRepel);
            else
                UpgradeData.object[Rod.rod_id, Rod.slot_id] = Lure.fish.object_id;
        }
        else
        {
            instance_create(0, 0, Message_NoFishOnRod);
            allowPurchase = 0;
        }
    }
    
    if (allowPurchase)
    {
        Girl.dollars -= cost;
        UpgradeData.upg[Rod.rod_id, Rod.slot_id] = card_id;
        upgrade_was_purchased(card_id);
    }
}
else
{
    allowPurchase = 0;
    instance_create(0, 0, Message_MoreMoney);
}

with (UpgradeMenu)
    instance_destroy();

if (allowPurchase)
    instance_create(0, 0, UpgradeSlotMenu);

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_buy_lure.gml =====
if (Lure.state == 0 && icon_Lures.unlocked && LureData.unlocked[argument0])
{
    if (argument0 == 1)
    {
        if (Lure.hasRock)
        {
            with (Lure.rock)
                pal = -1;
        }
    }
    
    allowPurchase = 1;
    cost = LureData.cost[argument0] * Girl.gear_lure_scalar[argument0];
    
    if (!instance_exists(LureData.item[argument0]) || LureData.item[argument0] == 295 || LureData.item[argument0] == 289)
    {
        if (Girl.dollars >= cost)
        {
            if (LureData.item[argument0] == 294 && instance_exists(BigHook))
                allowPurchase = 0;
            
            if (LureData.item[argument0] == 289 && Lure.hookedFishSize > 0)
            {
                if (Lure.hookedFishSize < 3)
                {
                    lure_fish_to_bait();
                }
                else
                {
                    instance_create(0, 0, Message_SellBigfish);
                    allowPurchase = 0;
                }
            }
            
            if (LureData.item[argument0] == 289 && instance_exists(Bomb))
            {
                if (!Rod.upgrades[18])
                    allowPurchase = 0;
                
                if (Bomb.stacks >= 5)
                    allowPurchase = 0;
            }
            
            if (allowPurchase)
            {
                Girl.dollars -= cost;
                instance_create(0, 0, LureData.item[argument0]);
                lure_was_purchased(argument0);
            }
        }
        else
        {
            instance_create(0, 0, Message_MoreMoney);
        }
    }
    
    with (LureMenu)
        instance_destroy();
}

if (!hasEquipment)
{
    if (hookedFishSize > 0)
    {
        if (fish.catalog_index >= 0 && fish.life >= 3)
        {
            val = CatalogData.weight[fish.display_size - 1, fish.catalog_index];
            CatalogData.weight[fish.display_size - 1, fish.catalog_index] = min(val, -5);
            
            if (fish.display_size > 3)
                CatalogData.weight[fish.display_size - 1, fish.catalog_index] = min(val, -10);

            {

                        failed = 1;
                }

            audio_play_sound(sfx_cat_unhappy, 0, false);
        }

    Girl.counter[0]++;
    Girl.castsSinceLastLure++;

{
    if (anchor > 0)
    {
        instance_destroy(anchor);

    collision_cooldown = 5;
    holding = argument0;
    
    if (holding > 0)

        if (holding.onBoat)

            holding.stick_target.fish_value -= holding.value;
            Girl.nFishToSell--;

            {
            }
            else
            {
                holding.stick_target.fish_count--;
                
                if (holding.stick_target.fish_count < 0)
                    holding.stick_target.fish_count = 0;
            }
        }
        
        if (holding.state == 3)
        {
            with (Lure)
                fish_release();
        }
        
        with (holding)
        {
            fish_kill_path();
            fish_started_fleeing();
        }
        
        holding_big = 0;
        holding = touching;
        holding_saved_depth = holding.depth;
        holding.stuck = 0;
        holding.onBoat = 0;
        holding.depth = depth - 0.1;
        holding.state = 4;
        holding.stick_target = id;
        holding.stick_dist = 40;
        holding.stick_angle = 0;
        holding.stick_x_offset = 0;
        holding.stick_y_offset = 3;
        hold_flip = holding.image_yscale;

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_check_lunge_catswim.gml =====
if (!instance_exists(CatSwim))
    return 0;

if (CatSwim.holding <= 0)
    return 0;

if (!instance_exists(CatSwim.holding))
    return 0;

if (CatSwim.holding_gear)
    return 0;

var range = base_lunge_offset + (Lure.lunge_scale * LUNGE_RANGE_START);

if (state == 13)
    range = base_lunge_offset + (Lure.lunge_scale * LUNGE_RANGE_STOP);

var dist = point_distance(x, y, CatSwim.x, CatSwim.y);

if ((dist * dist) > (range * range))
    return 0;

if (object_index == Turgeon.object_index && CatSwim.holding.object_index == Sibilfin.object_index)
    return 0;

if (CatSwim.holding.size >= size)
    return 0;

if (CatSwim.holding.size < 1)
    return 0;

if (state != 13 && !bumped)
{
    if (CatSwim.holding.size == 1 && size == 3)
        return 0;
}

if (CatSwim.holding.life <= 0)
    return 0;

if (!CatSwim.in_water)
    return 0;

if (collision_line(x, y, CatSwim.holding.x, CatSwim.holding.y, Collidable, true, true))
    return 0;

return 1;


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_check_lunge.gml =====
if (point_distance(x, y, Lure.x, Lure.y) < (base_lunge_offset + (Lure.lunge_scale * LUNGE_RANGE_START * lunge_mod)) && Lure.hookedFishSize < size && Lure.hookedFishSize < 3 && (Lure.size > 0 || Lure.baitTarget > 0) && (Lure.hookedFishSize == 0 || Lure.fish.life > 0) && Lure.in_the_water && Lure.state != 3 && !Lure.hasEquipment && ((object_index == Mudback.object_index || object_index == Orwellian.object_index) || !collision_line(x, y, Lure.x, Lure.y, CaveRock, true, false)) && (size > 1 || Lure.size < 3 || !Rod.upgrades[4]))

light_width -= (1200 * (1 - LightingSystem.overcast_coefficient));

        Lure.hookedFishSize = size;

        Lure.rock = -1;
        Lure.size = 1;
        Lure.fish = id;
        Lure.hookedFishSize = size;
        state = 3;
    }
    else
    {
        state = 5;
        alarm[0] = NOTHUNGRY_TIMER;
    }

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_fish_in_net.gml =====
Girl.noFishInNet = 0;
target_x = NetCenter.x;
target_y = NetCenter.y;

if (random(1) > 0.98 || Net.net_distance < 100)
{
    go_top = 0;
    go_bot = 0;
}

if (random(1) > 0.92)
    go_top = 1;

if (random(1) > 0.92)
    go_bot = 1;

weight_scale = sin((start_x + current_time) / 1000) + 1;

if (go_top && !go_bot)
{
    target_x = ((weight_scale * Net.top.x) + NetCenter.x) / (weight_scale + 1);
    target_y = ((weight_scale * Net.top.y) + NetCenter.y) / (weight_scale + 1);
}
else if (go_bot && !go_top)
{
    target_x = ((weight_scale * Net.bot.x) + NetCenter.x) / (weight_scale + 1);
    target_y = ((weight_scale * Net.bot.y) + NetCenter.y) / (weight_scale + 1);
}

speed *= (0.98 * clamp(Net.net_distance / 300, 0, 1));
hspeed += ((target_x - x) * clamp(50 / Net.net_distance, 0.002, 0.0135));
vspeed += ((target_y - y) * clamp(50 / Net.net_distance, 0.002, 0.0135));
girl_delta = Girl.x - NetCenter.xprevious;

if (girl_delta < 0)
    x += (girl_delta * 0.007);

x += (NetCenter.x - NetCenter.xprevious);
y += (NetCenter.y - NetCenter.yprevious);

if (nodes > 0 && is_array(nodes))
{
    target_x = nodes[target].x;
    target_y = nodes[target].y;
    
    if (point_distance(x, y, target_x, target_y) < node_distance_before_flip)
    {
        target++;
        
        if (target > (nodecount - 1))
            target = 0;
    }
    
    hspeed += ((target_x - x) * acceleration);
    vspeed += ((target_y - y) * acceleration);
    
    if (speed < min_speed)
        speed = min_speed;
    
    speed *= friction_coefficient;
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_fish_predraw_switchcase.gml =====
frames_since_last_flip++;

switch (state)
{
    case 0:
    case 1:
    case 2:
    case 5:
    case 8:
    case 9:
    case 7:
    case 10:
    case 13:
    case 15:
        if (autoflip)
        {
            if (speed > 1 || state != 5)
                angle = point_direction(xprevious, yprevious, x, y);
            else
                angle = 90 + (sign(image_yscale) * 90);
            
            if (set_angle)
                image_angle = angle - 180;
            
            if (!cheeky && frames_since_last_flip > 30)
            {
                if (angle < 90 || angle > 270)
                {
                    if (sign(image_yscale) > 0)
                        frames_since_last_flip = 0;
                    
                    image_yscale = -1 * image_xscale;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (sign(image_yscale) < 0)
                        frames_since_last_flip = 0;
                    
                    image_yscale = 1 * image_xscale;
                }
            }
        }
        
        image_yscale = sign(image_yscale) * image_xscale;
        break;
    
    case 3:
        if (Lure.state == 1)
        {
            if (x < Lure.tether_x)
            {
                image_yscale = -1 * image_xscale;
                canThrash = 0;
            }
        }
        
        break;
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_increment_count_for_name.gml =====
with (CatalogData)
{
    i = array_height_2d(name) - 1;
    
    while (i >= 0)
    {
        j = array_length_2d(name, i) - 1;
        
        while (j >= 0)
        {
            if (argument0 == name[i, j])
            {
                if (Girl.RealismMode && i > 1)
                    steam_set_achievement("rf_" + string(i) + "_" + string(j));
                
                if (count[i, j] < 0)
                    count[i, j] = 0;
                
                count[i, j]++;
            }
            
            j--;
        }
        
        i--;
    }
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_millis_to_time_string.gml =====

minutes = floor(millis / 60000);

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_nav_get_random_connected_node.gml =====
var nearestNode = argument[0];
var previousNode = argument[1];
var availableConnections = ds_list_create();

for (var i = 0; i < ds_list_size(nearestNode.edges); i++)
{
    var edge = ds_list_find_value(nearestNode.edges, i);
    
    if (edge.endNode != previousNode && edge.endNode != nearestNode)
        ds_list_add(availableConnections, edge.endNode);
    
    if (edge.startNode != previousNode && edge.startNode != nearestNode)
        ds_list_add(availableConnections, edge.startNode);
}

var randomNode = -1;

if (ds_list_size(availableConnections) > 0)
{
    var randomIndex = irandom_range(0, ds_list_size(availableConnections) - 1);
    randomNode = ds_list_find_value(availableConnections, randomIndex);
}

ds_list_destroy(availableConnections);
return randomNode;


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_new_quest.gml =====
with (QuestEngine)
{
    questWeights[0] = 0;
    questWeights[2] = 0;
    questWeights[3] = 0;
    questWeights[1] = 0;
    questWeights[4] = 0;
    questWeights[5] = 0;
    questWeights[6] = 0;
    questWeights[7] = 10;
    
    if (Girl.counter[8] < 8)
    {
        questWeights[6] = 10;
        questWeights[7] = 0;
    }
    
    if (Girl.level < 15)
    {
        if (Girl.bestRod > 0)
        {
            if (Rod.n_upgrades == 0 && !instance_exists(UpgradeQuest) && Girl.counter[6] < 3)
                questWeights[5] = 200;
            
            if (Girl.bestRod < 2 && CatalogData.count[2, 0] >= 1 && icon_Rods.unlocked && !instance_exists(RodQuest))
            {
                progressToNextRod = Girl.dollars / RodData.cost[Girl.bestRod + 1];
                questWeights[2] = 0 + (100 * power(max(0, progressToNextRod - 0.5), 3));
            }
            
            if (!instance_exists(LureQuest) && icon_Lures.unlocked)
                questWeights[1] = max(0, Girl.castsSinceLastLure - 12);
            
            if (!instance_exists(DistQuest) && !instance_exists(DepthQuest))
            {
                questWeights[3] = 1;
                
                if (Girl.furthest_cast > 3)
                    questWeights[3] = 0;
            }
        }
        else if (Girl.counter[5] > 0)
        {
            if (!instance_exists(RodQuest))
            {
                questWeights[7] = 0;
                questWeights[6] = 0;
                questWeights[2] = 10;
            }
            else
            {
                questWeights[7] = 0;
                questWeights[6] = 0;
                questWeights[3] = 0;
                questWeights[5] = 10;
            }
        }
    }
    
    sum = 0;
    
    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++)
        sum += questWeights[i];
    
    roll = random(sum);
    sum = 0;
    
    for (bin = 0; bin < 8; bin++)
    {
        sum += questWeights[bin];
        
        if (roll < sum)
            break;
    }
    
    start_quest(bin, 0);
}

with (SaveManager)
    event_perform(ev_other, ev_user1);

var hue_mean = argument[1];
var sat_range = argument[2];
var sat_mean = argument[3];
var val_range = argument[4];
var val_mean = argument[5];
var rand = random(1);
var hue = ((rand * hue_range) - (hue_range * 0.5)) + hue_mean;

if (hue < 0)
    hue = 255 - hue;

if (hue > 255)
    hue -= 255;

rand = random(1);
var sat = ((rand * sat_range) - (sat_range * 0.5)) + sat_mean;

if (sat < 0)
    sat = 0;

if (sat > 255)
    sat = 255;

rand = random(1);
var val = ((rand * val_range) - (val_range * 0.5)) + val_mean;

if (val < 0)
    val = 0;

if (val > 255)
    val = 255;

image_blend = make_color_hsv(hue, sat, val);

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_sell_fish.gml =====
var dollars_before = Girl.dollars;

with (Boat)
    event_perform(ev_other, ev_user3);

with (Fish)
{
    if (state == 4)
        fish_die();
    
    if (state == 15)
        fish_die();
    
    if (state == 3 && Rod.state == 0)
    {
        Girl.nFishToSell++;
        
        with (Lure)
            fish_release();
        
        fish_die();
    }
}

with (Net)
    instance_destroy();

Analytics.fish_caught = Girl.fishCaught;
Analytics.fish_sold = Girl.nFishToSell;
Analytics.cash_earned = Girl.dollars - dollars_before;

with (Analytics)
    event_perform(ev_other, ev_user6);

    Boat.fish_count = 0;
    Boat.fish_value = 0;
}

Cliff.fish_value = 0;

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_set_best_life_for_name.gml =====
with (CatalogData)
{
    i = array_height_2d(name) - 1;
    
    while (i >= 0)
    {
        j = array_length_2d(name, i) - 1;
        
        while (j >= 0)
        {
            if (argument0 == name[i, j])
            {
                if (life[i, j] < argument1)
                    life[i, j] = argument1;
            }
            
            j--;
        }
        
        i--;
    }
}

if (current_month == 4 && current_day >= 29)
    return 1;

    return 1;

{
    if (current_weekday == 0 || current_weekday == 6)
        return 1;
}

{
    if (current_day == 9)
        return 1;
}

    return 1;

if (current_month == 12 && current_day > 20 && (current_hour % 4) == 0)
    return 1;

return 0;

        q.type = 3;
        q.index = index;

        q.type = 3;

            break;

            q.fish_object_1 = 41;
            q.goal = 1;
            q.cash = 5000;
            break;
        
        case 53:
            q.fish = "Torby";
            q.fish_object_1 = 42;
            q.goal = 1;
            q.cash = 5000;
            
            if (Girl.torby_are_extinct)
            {
                if (Girl.counter[8] < n_quests)
                {
                    Girl.counter[8]++;
                    index++;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                break;
            }
        
        case 54:
            q.fish = "Clownfish";
            q.fish_object_1 = 32;
            q.goal = 1;
            q.cash = 5000;
            break;
        
        case 55:
            q.fish = "Moga";
            q.fish_object_1 = 47;
            q.goal = 1;
            q.cash = 10000;
            break;
        
        case 56:
            q.fish = "Bonefish";
            q.fish_object_1 = 46;
            q.goal = 1;
            q.cash = 5000;
            break;
        
        case 57:
            q.fish = "Maw";
            q.fish_object_1 = 29;
            q.goal = 1;
            q.cash = 10000;
            break;
        
        case 58:
            q.fish = "Snoutfish";
            q.fish_object_1 = 43;
            q.goal = 1;
            q.cash = 5000;
            break;
    }
    
    if (!allowDuplicates)
    {
        if (index < n_quests)
        {
            if (get_life_for_name(q.fish) >= 3)
            {
                Girl.counter[8]++;
                index = Girl.counter[8];
                success = 0;
            }
            else
            {
                success = 1;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            index = irandom_range(18, n_quests);
            allowDuplicates = 1;
            success = 0;
        }
    }
    else
    {
        success = 1;
    }
}

q.type = 6;
q.index = index;
unhide_entry_for_name(q.fish);

with (q)
    event_perform(ev_other, ev_user1);

return q;

    value = (argument0 % 1000) * 100;

        event_perform(ev_other, ev_user0);
    
    if (CatalogData.quest_chosen)
    {
        size = CatalogData.t_size;
        fish_id = CatalogData.t_fish_id;
        count = CatalogData.t_count;
        value = CatalogData.t_value;
        index = (size * 10000000) + (fish_id * 100000) + (count * 1000) + (value / 100);

        index = -5;
    }

}
else
{
    return -5;
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_b64_to_bytes.gml =====
var b64 = argument0;
var len = string_length(b64);
var pad = "=";
var tab = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
var str = "";

while (string_length(b64) % 4)
    b64 += pad;

for (var i = 0; i < len; i += 4)
{
    var bin;
    bin[0] = string_pos(string_char_at(b64, i + 1), tab) - 1;
    bin[1] = string_pos(string_char_at(b64, i + 2), tab) - 1;
    bin[2] = string_pos(string_char_at(b64, i + 3), tab) - 1;
    bin[3] = string_pos(string_char_at(b64, i + 4), tab) - 1;
    str += chr((255 & (bin[0] << 2)) | (bin[1] >> 4));
    
    if (bin[2] >= 0)
        str += chr((255 & (bin[1] << 4)) | (bin[2] >> 2));
    
    if (bin[3] >= 0)
        str += chr((255 & (bin[2] << 6)) | bin[3]);
}

return str;


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_bytes_to_b64.gml =====
var str = argument0;
var len = string_length(str);
var pad = "=";
var tab = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
var b64 = "";

for (var i = 0; i < len; i += 3)
{
    var bin;
    bin[0] = ord(string_char_at(str, i + 1));
    bin[1] = ord(string_char_at(str, i + 2));
    bin[2] = ord(string_char_at(str, i + 3));
    b64 += string_char_at(tab, 1 + (bin[0] >> 2));
    b64 += string_char_at(tab, 1 + (((bin[0] & 3) << 4) | (bin[1] >> 4)));
    
    if ((i + 1) >= len)
        b64 += pad;
    else
        b64 += string_char_at(tab, 1 + (((bin[1] & 15) << 2) | (bin[2] >> 6)));
    
    if ((i + 2) >= len)
        b64 += pad;
    else
        b64 += string_char_at(tab, 1 + (bin[2] & 63));
}

return b64;

    exit;

ds_map_secure_save(global.tmc_sini_map, global.tmc_sini_file);
ds_map_destroy(global.tmc_sini_map);
global.tmc_sini_map = -1;

if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
    show_debug_message("CLOSE INI: ");

    ds_map_delete(global.tmc_sini_map, key);

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_open.gml =====
global.tmc_ini_extra_key = "TMC_INI";
global.tmc_sini_file = argument0;
global.tmc_sini_map = -1;
global.tmc_sini_encoding = 1;
global.tmc_sini_debug = 1;

if (file_exists(argument0))
{
    global.tmc_sini_map = ds_map_secure_load(argument0);
    global.tmc_sini_encoding = tmc_sini_read_real("TMC_SINI", "ENCODING", 0);
    
    if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
        show_debug_message("OPEN INI FOUND: " + argument0);
}

if (!ds_exists(global.tmc_sini_map, ds_type_map))
{
    global.tmc_sini_map = ds_map_create();
    
    if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
        show_debug_message("OPEN INI NOT FOUND: " + argument0);
}


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_read_real.gml =====
return real(tmc_sini_read_string(argument0, argument1, argument2));


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_read_string.gml =====
var key = string(argument0) + ":" + string(argument1);
var ret = string(argument2);

if (!ds_exists(global.tmc_sini_map, ds_type_map))
    return ret;

if (ds_map_exists(global.tmc_sini_map, key))
{
    ret = string(ds_map_find_value(global.tmc_sini_map, key));
    
    if (ret != "")
    {
        if (string(argument0) == "TMC_SINI")
        {
            if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
                show_debug_message("READ TMC_SINI: " + key + "=" + ret);
        }
        else if (global.tmc_sini_encoding == 1)
        {
            if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
                show_debug_message("READ ENCODED: " + key + "=" + ret);
            
            ret = tmc_sini_b64_to_bytes(tmc_sini_vigenere_ascii(tmc_sini_b64_to_bytes(ret), sha1_string_unicode(key + global.tmc_ini_extra_key), 0));
            
            if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
                show_debug_message("READ DECODED: " + key + "=" + ret);
        }
        else
        {
            if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
                show_debug_message("OLD READ ENCODED: " + key + "=" + ret);
            
            ret = tmc_sini_b64_to_bytes(ret);
            
            if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
                show_debug_message("OLD READ DECODED: " + key + "=" + ret);
        }
    }
}

return ret;


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_section_delete.gml =====
if (!ds_exists(global.tmc_sini_map, ds_type_map))
    exit;

var key = string(argument0) + ":";
var size = ds_map_size(global.tmc_sini_map);
var fkey = ds_map_find_first(global.tmc_sini_map);
var l = ds_list_create();

if (string_count(key, string(fkey)))
    ds_list_add(l, fkey);

for (var i = 0; i < (size - 1); i += 1)
{
    fkey = ds_map_find_next(global.tmc_sini_map, fkey);
    
    if (string_count(key, string(fkey)))
        ds_list_add(l, fkey);
}

size = ds_list_size(l);

for (var i = 0; i < size; i++)
    ds_map_delete(global.tmc_sini_map, ds_list_find_value(l, i));

ds_list_destroy(l);
return 0;


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_section_exists.gml =====
if (!ds_exists(global.tmc_sini_map, ds_type_map))
    return 0;

var key = string(argument0) + ":";
var size = ds_map_size(global.tmc_sini_map);
var fkey = ds_map_find_first(global.tmc_sini_map);

if (string_count(key, string(fkey)))
    return 1;

for (var i = 0; i < (size - 1); i += 1)
{
    fkey = ds_map_find_next(global.tmc_sini_map, fkey);
    
    if (string_count(key, string(fkey)))
        return 1;
}

return 0;


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_vigenere_ascii.gml =====
var in = argument0;
var key = argument1;
var mode = argument2;
var out = "";
var inLen = string_length(in);
var keyLen = string_length(key);
var loVal = 32;
var hiVal = 126;
var span = (hiVal - loVal) + 1;

for (var pos = 0; pos < inLen; pos += 1)
{
    var inChar = string_char_at(in, pos + 1);
    var keyChar = string_char_at(key, (pos % keyLen) + 1);
    var inVal = min(max(loVal, ord(inChar)), hiVal) - loVal;
    var keyVal = min(max(loVal, ord(keyChar)), hiVal) - loVal;
    var outVal;
    
    if (mode)
        outVal = ((inVal + keyVal) % span) + loVal;
    else
        outVal = (((span + inVal) - keyVal) % span) + loVal;
    
    var outChar = chr(outVal);
    out = out + outChar;
}

return out;


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_write_real.gml =====
tmc_sini_write_string(argument0, argument1, argument2);


===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_tmc_sini_write_string.gml =====
if (!ds_exists(global.tmc_sini_map, ds_type_map))
    exit;

var key = string(argument0) + ":" + string(argument1);
var val = string(argument2);

if (val != "")
{
    if (string(argument0) == "TMC_SINI")
    {
        if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
            show_debug_message("WRITE TMC_SINI: " + key + "=" + val);
    }
    else if (global.tmc_sini_encoding == 1)
    {
        if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
            show_debug_message("WRITE VAL: " + key + "=" + val);
        
        val = tmc_sini_bytes_to_b64(tmc_sini_vigenere_ascii(tmc_sini_bytes_to_b64(val), sha1_string_unicode(key + global.tmc_ini_extra_key), 1));
        
        if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
            show_debug_message("WRITE ENCODED VAL: " + key + "=" + val);
    }
    else
    {
        if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
            show_debug_message("OLD WRITE VAL: " + key + "=" + val);
        
        val = tmc_sini_bytes_to_b64(val);
        
        if (global.tmc_sini_debug)
            show_debug_message("OLD WRITE VAL DECODED: " + key + "=" + val);
    }
}

if (ds_map_exists(global.tmc_sini_map, key))
    ds_map_replace(global.tmc_sini_map, key, val);
else
    ds_map_add(global.tmc_sini_map, key, val);

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_unhide_entry_for_name.gml =====
with (CatalogData)
{
    i = array_height_2d(name) - 1;
    
    while (i >= 0)
    {
        j = array_length_2d(name, i) - 1;
        
        while (j >= 0)
        {
            if (argument0 == name[i, j])
            {
                if (count[i, j] < 0)
                    count[i, j] = 0;
            }
            
            j--;
        }
        
        i--;
    }
}

===== FILE: All code cgf/gml_Script_vector_project_magnitude.gml =====
var alpha_x = argument0;
var alpha_y = argument1;
var beta_x = argument2;
var beta_y = argument3;
var dir = point_direction(0, 0, beta_x, beta_y);
beta_x = lengthdir_x(1, dir);
beta_y = lengthdir_y(1, dir);
var over = dot_product(alpha_x, alpha_y, beta_x, beta_y);
var under = dot_product(beta_x, beta_y, beta_x, beta_y);
return (over / under) * beta_x;
